        <body>
            <div n="6" type="sthāna">

                <head>[uttaratantram]</head>



                <div n="39" type="adhyāya">
                    <head>ekonacatvāriṃśattamo 'dhyāyaḥ | </head>

                   <pb facs="754" n="670ba"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.1"><l> athāto jvarapratiṣedhaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.3"><l> yenāmṛtam apāṃ madhyād uddhṛtaṃ pūrvajanmani |
                        <caesura/>
                         yato 'maratvaṃ saṃprāptās tridaśās tridiveśvarāt || </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="755" n="671a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.4"><l> śiṣyās taṃ devam āsīnaṃ papracchuḥ suśrutādayaḥ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         vraṇasyopadravāḥ proktā vraṇinām apy ataḥ param || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.5"><l> samāsād vyāsataś caiva brūhi no bhiṣajāṃ vara |
                        <caesura/>
                         upadraveṇa juṣṭasya vraṇaḥ kṛcchreṇa sidhyati || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.6"><l> upadravās tu vraṇinaḥ kṛcchrasādhyāḥ
                        prakīrtitāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         prakṣīṇabalamāṃsasya śeṣadhātuparikṣayāt || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.7ab"><l> tasmād upadravān kṛtsnān brūhi naḥ
                        sacikitsitān | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.7cd"><l> sarvakāyacikitsāsu ye dṛṣṭāḥ paramarṣiṇā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.8"><l> teṣāṃ tad vacanaṃ śrutvā prābravīd bhiṣajāṃ
                        varaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         jvaram ādau pravakṣyāmi sa rogānīkarāṭ smṛtaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.9"><l> rudrakopāgnisaṃbhūtaḥ sarvabhūtapratāpanaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         tais tair nāmabhir anyeṣāṃ sattvānāṃ parikīrtyate || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.10"><l> janmādau nidhane caiva prāyo viśati dehinam |
                        <caesura/>
                         ataḥ sarvavikārāṇām ayaṃ rājā prakīrtitaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.11"><l> ṛte devamanuṣyebhyo nānyo viṣahate tu tam |
                        <caesura/>
                         karmaṇā labhate yasmād devatvaṃ mānusād api || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.12"><l> punaś caiva cyutaḥ svargān mānuṣyam anuvartate
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         tasmāt te devabhāvena sahante mānuṣā jvaram || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.13"><l> śeṣāḥ sarve vipadyante tairyagyonā jvarārditāḥ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         svedāvarodhaḥ santāpaḥ sarvāṅgagrahaṇaṃ tathā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.14"><l> vikārā yugapad yasmin jvaraḥ sa parikīrtitaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                        <pb facs="756" n="672a"/> doṣaiḥ pṛthak samastaiś ca dvandvair                         āgantur eva ca || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.15"><l> anekakāraṇotpannaḥ smṛtas tv aṣṭavidho jvaraḥ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         doṣāḥ prakupitāḥ sveṣu kāleṣu svaiḥ prakopaṇaiḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.16"><l> vyāpya deham aśeṣeṇa jvaram āpādayanti hi |
                        <caesura/>
                         duṣṭāḥ svahetubhir doṣāḥ prāpyām āśayam ūṣmaṇā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.17"><l> sahitā rasam āgatya rasasvedapravāhiṇām |
                        <caesura/>
                         srotasāṃ mārgam āvṛtya mandīkṛtya hutāśanam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.18"><l> nirasya bahir ūṣmāṇaṃ paktisthānāc ca kevalam
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         śarīraṃ samabhivyāpya svakāleṣu jvarāgamam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.19ab"><l> janayaty atha vṛddhiṃ vā svavarṇaṃ ca
                        tvagādiṣu |</l></lg>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.19cd"><l> mithyāti<pc> </pc>yuktair api ca snehādyaiḥ
                        karmabhir nṛṇām || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.20"><l> vividhād abhighātāc ca rogotthānāt prapākataḥ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         śramāt kṣayād ajīrṇāc ca viṣāt sātmyartuparyayāt || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.21"><l> oṣadhīpuṣpagandhāc ca śokān nakṣatrapīḍayā |
                        <caesura/>
                         abhicārābhiśāpābhyāṃ manobhūtābhiśaṅkayā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.22"><l> strīṇām apaprajātānāṃ prajātānāṃ tathāhitaiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         stanyāvataraṇe caiva jvaro doṣaiḥ pravartate || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.23"><l> tair vegavadbhir bahudhā samudbhrāntair
                        vimārgagaiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         vikṣipyamāṇo 'ntaragnir bhavaty āśu bahiś caraḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.24"><l> ruṇaddhi cāpy apāṃ dhātuṃ yasmāt tasmāj
                        jvarāturaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         bhavaty atyuṣṇagātraś ca jvaritastena cocyate || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.25"><l> śramo 'ratirvivarṇatvaṃ vairasyaṃ nayanaplavaḥ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         icchādveṣau muhuś cāpi śītavātātapādiṣu || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.26"><l> jṛmbhāṅgamardo gurutā romaharṣo 'rucis tamaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         apraharṣaś ca śītaṃ ca bhavaty utpatsyati jvare || </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="757" n="673a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.27"><l> sāmānyato viśeṣāt tu jṛmbhātyarthaṃ samīraṇāt
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         pittānnayanayor dāhaḥ kaphānnannābhinandanam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.28"><l> sarvaliṅgasamavāyaḥ sarvadoṣaprakopaje |
                        <caesura/>
                         dvayor dvayos tu rūpeṇa saṃsṛṣṭaṃ dvandvajaṃ viduḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.29"><l> vepathur viṣamo vegaḥ kaṇṭhauṣṭhapariśoṣaṇam |
                        <caesura/>
                         nidrānāśaḥ kṣutaḥ stambho gātrāṇāṃ raukṣyam eva ca || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.30"><l> śirohṛdgātrarugvaktravairasyaṃ baddhaviṭkatā |
                        <caesura/>
                         jṛmbhādhmānṃa tathā śūlaṃ bhavaty anilaje jvare || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.31"><l> vegastīkṣṇo 'tisāraś ca nidrālpatvaṃ tathā
                        vamiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         kaṇṭhauṣṭhamukhanāsānāṃ pākaḥ svedaś ca jāyate || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.32"><l> pralāpaḥ kaṭutā vaktre mūrcchā dāho madastṛṣā
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         pītaviṇmūtranetratvaṃ paittike bhrama eva ca || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.33"><l> gauravaṃ śītamutkleśo romaharṣo 'tinidratā |
                        <caesura/>
                         srotorodho rugalpatvaṃ praseko madhurāsyatā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.34"><l> nātyuṣṇagātratā chardiraṅgasādo 'vipākatā |
                        <caesura/>
                         pratiśyāyo 'ruciḥ kāsaḥ kaphaje 'kṣṇoś ca śuklatā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.35"><l> nidrānāśo bhramaḥ śvāsastandrā suptāṅgatāruciḥ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         tṛṣṇā moho madaḥ stambho dāhaḥ śītaṃ hṛdi vyathā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.36"><l> paktiś cireṇa doṣāṇāmunmādaḥ śyāvadantatā |
                        <caesura/>
                         rasanā paruṣā kṛṣṇā sandhimūrdhāsthijā rujaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.37"><l> nirbhugne kaluṣe netre karṇau śabdaruganvitau
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         pralāpaḥ srotasāṃ pākaḥ kūjanaṃ cetanācyutiḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.38"><l> svedamūtrapurīṣāṇām alpaśaḥ sucirāt srutiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         sarvaje sarvaliṅgāni viśeṣaṃ cātra me śṛṇu ||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.39"><l> nātyuṣṇaśīto 'lpasaṃjño bhrāntaprekṣī
                        hatasvaraḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         kharajihvaḥ śuṣkakaṇṭhaḥ svedaviṇmūtravarjitaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.40"><l> sāsro nirbhugnahṛdayo bhaktadveṣī hataprabhaḥ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         śvasannipatitaḥ śete pralāpopadravāyutaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.41"><l> tam abhinyāsam ity āhur hataujasam athāpare |
                        <caesura/>
                        <pb facs="758" n="674a"/> sannipātajvaraṃ kṛcchram asādhyam apare                         viduḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.42"><l> nidropetam abhinyāsaṃ kṣīṇamenaṃ hataujasam |
                        <caesura/>
                         saṃnyastagātraṃ saṃnyāsaṃ vidyāt sarvātmake jvare || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.43"><l> ojo visraṃsate yasya pittānilasamucchrayāt |
                        <caesura/>
                         sa gātrastambhaśītābhyāṃ śayanepsuracetanaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.44"><l> api jāgrat svapan jantustandrāluś ca
                        pralāpavān |
                        <caesura/>
                         saṃhṛṣṭaromā srastāṅgo mandasantāpavedanaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.45"><l> ojonirodhajaṃ tasya jānīyāt kuśalo bhis ak |
                        <caesura/>
                         saptame vidase prāpte daśame dvādaśe 'pi vā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.46"><l> punar dhorataro bhūtvā praśamaṃ yāti hanti vā
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         dvidoṣocchrāyaliṅgās tu dvandvajās trividhāḥ smṛtāḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.47"><l> tṛṣṇā mūrcchā bhramo dāhaḥ svapnanāśaḥ
                        śirorujā |
                        <caesura/>
                         kaṇṭhāsyaśoṣo vamathū romaharṣo 'rucis tathā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.48"><l> parvbhedaś ca jṛmbhā ca vātapittajvarākṛtiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         staimityaṃ parvaṇaṃ bhedo nidrā gauravam eva ca || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.49"><l> śirograhaḥ pratiśyāyaḥ kāsaḥ svedapravartanam
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         santāpo madhyavegaś ca vātaśleṣmajvarākṛtiḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.50"><l> liptātiktāsyatā tandrā mohaḥ kāso 'rucistṛṣā |
                        <caesura/>
                         muhurdāho muhuḥ śītaṃ śleṣmapittajvarākṛtiḥ ||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.50.1"><l> ( |
                        <caesura/>
                         jṛmbhādhmānamadotkampaparvabhedaparikṣayāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         tṛṭpralāpābhitāpāḥ syurjvare mārutapaittike || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.50.2"><l> śūlakāsakakotkleśaśītavepathupīnasāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         gauravāruciviṣṭambhā vātaśleṣmasamudbhave || </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="759" n="675a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.50.3"><l> śītadāhārucistambhasvedamohamadabhramāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         kāsāṅgasādahṛllāsā bhavanti kaphapaittike ||) </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.51"><l> kṛśānāṃ jvaramuktānāṃ mithyāhāravihāriṇām |
                        <caesura/>
                         doṣaḥ svalpo 'pi saṃvṛddho dehinām anileritaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.52"><l> satatānyed yuṣkatryākhyacāturthān sapralepakān
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         kaphasthānavibhāgena yathāsaṃkhyaṃ karoti hi || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.53"><l> ahorātrādahorātrāt sthānāt sthānaṃ prapadyate
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         tataś cāmāśayaṃ prāpya doṣaḥ kuryājjvaraṃ nṛṇām || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.54"><l> tathā pralepako jñeyaḥ śoṣiṇāṃ prāṇanāśanaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         duścikitsyatamo mandaḥ sukaṣṭo dhātuśoṣakṛt || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.55"><l> kaphasthāneṣu vā doṣastiṣṭan dvitricaturṣu vā
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         viparyayākhyān kurute viṣamān kṛcchrasādhanān || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.56"><l> paro hetuḥ svabhāvo vā viṣame kaiś cidīritaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         āgantuś canubandho hi prāyaśo viṣamajvare || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.57"><l> vātādhikatvāt pravadanti tajjñāstṛtīyakaṃ cāpi
                        caturthakaṃ ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         aupatyake madyasamudbhave ca hetuṃ jvare pittakṛtaṃ vadanti || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.58"><l> pralepakaṃ vātabalāsakaṃ ca kaphādhikatvena
                        vadanti tajjñāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         mūrcchānubandhā viṣam ajvarā ye prāyeṇa te dvandvasamutthitās tu
                        || </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="760" n="676a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.59"><l> tvaksthau śleṣmānilau śītamādau janayato jvare
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         tayoḥ praśāntayoḥ pittamante dāhaṃ karoti ca || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.60"><l> karoty ādau tathā pittaṃ tvaksthaṃ dāham atīva
                        ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         praśānte kurutas tasmiṃś chītam ante ca tāv api || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.61"><l> dvāv etau dāhaśītādī jvarau saṃsargajau smṛtau
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         dāhapūrvas tayoḥ kaṣṭaḥ kṛcchrasādhyaś ca sa smṛtaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.62"><l> prasaktaś cābhighātotthaś cetanāprabhavas tu
                        yaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         rātryahnoḥ ṣaṭsu kāleṣu kīrtiteṣu tathā purā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.63"><l> prasahya viṣamo 'bhyeti mānavaṃ bahudhā jvaraḥ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         sa cāpi viṣamo dehaṃ na kadācidvimuñcati || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.64"><l> glānigauravakārśyebhyaḥ sa yasmān na
                        pramucyate |
                        <caesura/>
                         vege tu samatikrānte gato 'yam iti lakṣyate || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.320"><l> ghṛtaṃ dvādaśarātrāt tu deyaṃ sarvajvareṣu ca
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         tenāntareṇāśayaṃ svaṃ gatā doṣā bhavanti hi || </l></lg>

                        <caesura/>

                        elājamodāmalakābhayākṣagāyatryariṣṭāsanaśālasārān |
                        <caesura/>
                         viḍaṅgabhallātakacitrakogrākaṭutrikāmbhodasurāṣṭrajāṃś ca || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.41.51"><l> paktvā jale tena paceddhi sarpistasmin
                        susiddhe tvavatārite ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         triṃśatpalānyatra sitopalāyā dattvā tugākṣīripalāni ṣaṭ ca || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.41.52"><l><pb facs="800" n="716a"/> prasthe ghṛtasya                         dviguṇaṃ ca dadyāt kṣaudraṃ
tato manthahataṃ vidadhyāt |
                        <caesura/>
                         palaṃ palaṃ prātarataḥ pralihya paścāt pibet kṣīramatandritaś ca
                        || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.41.53"><l> etad dhi medhyaṃ paramaṃ pavitraṃ
                        cakṣuṣyamāyuṣyamatho yaśasyam |
                        <caesura/>
                         yakṣmāṇamāśu vyapahanti caitat pāṇḍvāmayaṃ caiva bhagandaraṃ ca
                        || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.41.54ab"><l> śvāsaṃ ca hanti
                        svarabhedakāsahṛtplīhagulmagrahaṇīgadāṃś ca |</l></lg>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.41.54cd"><l> na cātra kiṃcit parivarjanīyaṃ rasāyanaṃ
                        caitadupāsyamānam || </l></lg>

                      <lg xml:id="SS.6.41.55"><l> plīhodaroktaṃ vihitaṃ ca sarpistrīṇyeva
                        cānyāni hitāni cātra |
                        <caesura/>
                         upadravāṃś ca svaravaikṛtādīn jayed yathāsvaṃ prasamīkṣya śāstram
                        || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.41.56"><l> ajāśakṛnmūtrapayoghṛtāsṛṅmāṃsālayāni
                        pratisevamānaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         snānādinānāvidhinā jahāti māsādaśeṣaṃ niyamena śoṣam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.41.57"><l> rasonayogaṃ vidhivat kṣayārtaḥ kṣīreṇa vā
                        nāgabalāprayogam |
                        <caesura/>
                         seveta vā māgadhikāvidhānaṃ tathopayogaṃ jatuno 'śmajasya || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.41.58"><l> śokaṃ striyaṃ krodhamasūyanaṃ ca tyajedudārān
                        viṣayān bhajeta |
                        <caesura/>
                         vaidyān dvijātīṃstridaśān gurūś ca vācaś ca puṇyāḥ
                        śṛṇuyādbijebhyaḥ ||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.41.trailer"><l>iti suśrutasaṃhitāyām uttaratantrāntargate
                        kāyacikitsātantre (tṛtīyo 'dhyāyaḥ, ādita) ekacatvāriṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ
                        ||41 ||</l></lg>

                        <caesura/>
                         uṣṇāmlakāñjikakṣīratoyaiḥ ślokasamāpanān || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.73"><l> yathākramaṃ vimiśrāṃś ca dvandve sarvāṃś ca
                        sarvaje |
                        <caesura/>
                         tathaiva sekāvagāhapradehābhyaṅgabhojanam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.74"><l> śiśirodakapūrṇānāṃ bhājanānāṃ ca dhāraṇam |
                        <caesura/>
                         vamanonmardanasvedalaṅghanakṣapaṇakriyāḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.75ab"><l> snehādiś ca karamaḥ sarvo viśeṣeṇopadiśyate
                        |</l></lg>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.75cd"><l> vallūraṃ mūlakaṃ matsyān śuṣkaśākāni
                        vaidalam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.76ab"><l> na khādedālukaṃ gulmī madhurāṇi phalāni ca
                        |</l></lg>
                    <pb facs="807" n="723a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.76cd"><l> vinā gulmena yacchūlaṃ gulmasthāneṣu jāyate
                        || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.77"><l> nidānaṃ tasya vakṣyāmi rūpaṃ ca sacikitsitam |
                        <caesura/>
                         vātamūtrapurīṣāṇāṃ nigrahādatibhojanāt || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.78"><l> ajīrṇādhyaśanāyāsaviruddhānnopasevanāt |
                        <caesura/>
                         pānīyapānāt kṣutkāle virūḍhānāṃ ca sevanāt || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.79"><l> piṣṭānnaśuṣkamāṃsānām upayogāttathaiva ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         evaṃvidhānāṃ dravyāṇāmanyeṣāṃ copasevanāt || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.80"><l> vāyuḥ prakupitaḥ koṣṭhe śūlaṃ saṃjanayed
                        bhṛśam |
                        <caesura/>
                         nirucchvāsī bhavettena vedanāpīḍito naraḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.81"><l> śaṅkusphoṭanavattasya yasmāttīvrāś ca vedanāḥ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         śūlāsaktasya lakṣyante tasmācchūlamihocyate || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.82"><l> nirāhārasya yasyaiva tīvraṃ śūlamudīryate |
                        <caesura/>
                         prastabdhagātro bhavati kṛcchreṇocchvasitīva ca || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.83"><l> vātamūtrapurīṣāṇi kṛcchreṇa kurute naraḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         etairliṅgairvijānīyācchūlaṃ vātasamudbhavam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.84"><l> tṛṣṇā dāho mado mūrcchā tīvraṃ śūlaṃ tathaiva
                        ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         śītābhikāmo bhavati śītenaiva praśāmyati || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.85"><l> etairliṅgairvijānīyācchūlaṃ pittasamudbhavam |
                        <caesura/>
                         śūlenotpīḍyamānasya hṛllāsa upajāyate || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.86"><l> atīva pūrṇakoṣṭhatvaṃ tathaiva gurugātratā |
                        <caesura/>
                         etacchleṣmasamutthasya śūlasyoktaṃ nidarśanam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.87"><l> sarvāṇi dṛṣṭvā rūpāṇi nirdiśetsānnipātikam |
                        <caesura/>
                         sannipātasamutthānamasādhyaṃ taṃ vinirdiśet || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.88"><l> śūlānāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ proktaṃ cikitsāṃ tu nibodha
                        me |
                        <caesura/>
                         āśukārī hi pavanastasmāt taṃ tvarayā jayet || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.89"><l> tasya śūlābhipannasya sveda eva sukhāvahaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         pāyasaiḥ kṛśarāpiṇḍaiḥ snigdhair vā piśitairhitaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.90"><l> trivṛcchākena vā snigdhamuṣṇaṃ bhiñjīta
                        bhojanam |
                        <caesura/>
                         cirabilvāṅkurān vāpi tailabhṛṣṭāṃs tu bhakṣayet || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.91"><l> vaihaṅgāṃś ca rasān snigdhān jāṅgalān
                        śūlapīḍitaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         yathālābhaṃ niṣeveta māṃsāni bilaśāyinām || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.92"><l> surā sauvīrakaṃ cukraṃ mastūdaśvittathā dadhi
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         sakālalavaṇaṃ peyaṃ śūle vātasamudbhave || </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="808" n="724a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.93"><l> kulatthayūṣo yuktāmlo lāvakīyūṣasaṃskṛtaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         sasaindhavaḥ samarico vātaśūlavināśanaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.94"><l> viḍaṅgaśigrukampillapathyāśyāmāmlavetasān |
                        <caesura/>
                         surasāmaśvamūtrīṃ ca sauvarcalayutān pibet || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.95"><l> madyena vātajaṃ śūlaṃ kṣipram eva praśāmyati |
                        <caesura/>
                         pṛthvīkājājicavikāyavānīvyoṣacitrakāḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.96"><l> pippalyaḥ pippalīmūlaṃ saindhavaṃ ceti
                        cūrṇayet |
                        <caesura/>
                         tāni cūrṇāni payasā pibet kāmbalikena vā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.97"><l> madhvāsavena cukreṇa surāsauvīrakeṇa vā |
                        <caesura/>
                         athavaitāni(athacaitāni) cūrṇāni mātuluṅgarasena vā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.98"><l> tathā badarayūṣeṇa bhāvitāni punaḥ punaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         tāni hiṅgupragāḍhāni saha śarkarayā pibet || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.99"><l> saha dāḍimasāreṇa vartiḥ kāryā bhiṣagjitā |
                        <caesura/>
                         sā vartirvātikaṃ śūlaṃ kṣipram eva vyapohati || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.100"><l> guḍatailena vā līḍhā pītā madyena vā punaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         bubhukṣāprabhave śūle laghu saṃtarpaṇaṃ hitam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.101"><l> uṣṇaiḥ kṣīrairyavāgūbhiḥ snigdhairmāṃsarasais
                        tathā |
                        <caesura/>
                         vātaśūle samutpanne rūkṣaṃ snigdhena bhojayet || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.102"><l> susaṃskṛtāḥ pradeyāḥ syurghṛtapūrā viśeṣataḥ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         vāruṇīṃ ca pibejjantus tathā saṃpadyate sukhī || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.103"><l> etadvātasamutthasya śūlasyoktaṃ cikitsitam |
                        <caesura/>
                         atha pittasamutthasya kriyāṃ vakṣyāmyataḥ param || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.104"><l> sa sukhaṃ chardayitvā tu pītvā śītodakaṃ
                        naraḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         śītalāni ca seveta sarvāṇyuṣṇāni varjayet || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.105"><l> maṇirājatatāmrāṇi bhājanāni ca sarvaśaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         vāripūrṇāni tānyasya śūlasyopari nikṣipet || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.106"><l> guḍaḥ śāliryavāḥ kṣīraṃ sarpiḥpānaṃ virecanam
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         jāṅgalāni ca māṃsāni bheṣajaṃ pittaśūlinām || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.107"><l> rasān seveta pittaghnān pittalāni vivarjayet
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         pālāśaṃ dhānvanaṃ vāpi pibedyūṣaṃ saśarkaram || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.108"><l> parūṣakāṇi mṛdvīkākharjūrodakajānyapi |
                        <caesura/>
                         tat pibec charkarāyuktaṃ pittaśūlanivāraṇam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.109"><l> aśane bhuktamātre tu prakopaḥ ślaiṣmikasya ca
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         vamanaṃ kārayettatra pippalīvāriṇā bhiṣak || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.110"><l> rūkṣaḥ svedaḥ prayojyaḥ syād anyāś coṣṇāḥ
                        kriyā hitāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         pippalī śṛṅgaveraṃ ca śleṣmaśūle bhiṣagjitam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.111"><l> pāṭhāṃ vacāṃ trikaṭukaṃ tathā kaṭukarohiṇīm |
                        <caesura/>
                         citrakasya ca niryūhe pibedyūṣaṃ sahārjakam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.112"><l><pb facs="809" n="725a"/> eraṇḍaphalamūlāni                         mūlaṃ gokṣurakasya ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         śālaparṇīṃ pṛśniparṇīṃ bṛhatīṃ kaṇṭakārikām || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.113"><l> dadyācchṛgālavinnāṃ ca sahadevāṃ tathaiva ca
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         mahāsahāṃ kṣudrasahāṃ mūlamikṣurakasya ca || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.114"><l> etat saṃbhṛtya saṃbhāraṃ jaladroṇe vipācayet
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         caturbhāgāvaśeṣaṃ tu yavakṣārayutaṃ pibet || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.115"><l> vātikaṃ paittikaṃ vāpi ślaiṣmikaṃ
                        sānnipātikam |
                        <caesura/>
                         prasahya nāśayec chūlaṃ chinnābhram iva mārutaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.116"><l> pippalī svarjikākṣāro yavāś citraka eva ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         sevyaṃ caitat samānīya bhasma kuryād vicakṣaṇaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.117"><l> tad uṣṇavāriṇā pītaṃ śleṣmaśūle bhiṣagjitam |
                        <caesura/>
                         ruṇaddhi mārutaṃ śleṣmā kukṣipārśvavyavasthitaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.118"><l> sa saṃruddhaḥ karotyāśu sādhmānaṃ
                        guḍguḍāyanam |
                        <caesura/>
                         sūcībhir iva nistodaṃ kṛcchrocchvāsī tadā naraḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.119"><l> nānnaṃ vāñchati no nidrām upaity
                        artinipīḍitaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         pārśvaśūlaḥ sa vijñeyaḥ kaphānilasamudbhavaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.120"><l> tatra puṣkaramūlāni hiṅgu sauvarcalaṃ viḍam |
                        <caesura/>
                         saindhavaṃ tumburuṃ pathyāṃ cūrṇaṃ kṛtvā tu pāyayet || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.121"><l> pārśvahṛdbastiśūleṣu yavakvāthena saṃyutam |
                        <caesura/>
                         sarpiḥ plīhodaroktaṃ vā ghṛtaṃ vā hiṅgusaṃyutam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.122"><l> bījapūrakasāraṃ vā payasā saha sādhitam |
                        <caesura/>
                         eraṇḍatailamathavā madyamas tu payorasaiḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.123"><l> bhojayec cāpi payasā jāṅgalena rasena vā |
                        <caesura/>
                         prakupyati yadā kukṣau vahnimākramya mārutaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.124"><l> tadāsya bhojanaṃ bhuktaṃ sopastambhaṃ na
                        pacyate |
                        <caesura/>
                         ucchvasity āmaśakṛtā śūlenāhanyate muhuḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.125"><l> naivāsane na śayane tiṣṭhan vā labhate sukham
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         kukṣiśūla iti khyāto vātādāmasamudbhavaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.126"><l> vamanaṃ kārayettatra laṅghayed vā yathābalam
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         saṃsargapācanaṃ kuryād amlair dīpanasaṃyutaiḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.127"><l> nāgaraṃ dīpyakaṃ cavyaṃ hiṅgu sauvarcalaṃ
                        viḍam |
                        <caesura/>
                         mātuluṅgasya bījāni tathā śyāmorubūkayoḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.128"><l> bṛhatyāḥ kaṇṭakāryāś ca kvāthaṃ śūlaharaṃ
                        pibet |
                        <caesura/>
                         vacā sauvarcalaṃ hiṅgu kuṣṭhaṃ sātiviṣābhayā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.129"><l> kuṭajasya ca bījāni sadyaḥśūlaharāṇi tu |
                        <caesura/>
                         virecane prayuñjīta jñātvā doṣabalābalam || </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="810" n="726a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.130"><l> snehabastīn nirūhāṃś ca kuryād doṣanibarhaṇān
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         upanāhāḥ snehasekā dhānyāmlapariṣecanam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.131"><l> avagāhāś ca śasyante yac cānyad api tad
                        dhitam |
                        <caesura/>
                         kaphapittāvaruddhas tu māruto rasamūrcchitaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.132"><l> hṛdisthaḥ kurute śūlamucchvāsārodhakaṃ param
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         sa hṛcchūla iti khyāto rasamārutasaṃbhavaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.133"><l> tatrāpi karmābhihitaṃ yadi uktaṃ hṛdvikāriṇām
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         saṃrodhāt kupito vāyuri bastim āvṛtya tiṣṭhati || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.134"><l> bastivaṅkṣaṇanābhīṣu tataḥ śūlo 'sya jāyate |
                        <caesura/>
                         viṇmūtravātasaṃrodhī bastiśūlaḥ sa mārutāt || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.135"><l> nābhyāṃ vaṅkṣaṇapārśveṣu kukṣau
                        meḍhrāntramardakaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         mūtramāvṛtya gṛhṇāti mūtraśūlaḥ sa mārutāt || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.136"><l> vāyuḥ prakupito yasya rūkṣāhārasya dehinaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         malaṃ ruṇaddhi koṣṭhasthaṃ mandīkṛtya tu pāvakam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.137"><l> śūlaṃ saṃjanayaṃstīvraṃ srotāṃsyāvṛtya tasya
                        hi |
                        <caesura/>
                         dakṣiṇaṃ yadi vā vāmaṃ kukṣimādāya jāyate || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.138"><l> sarvatra vardhate kṣipraṃ bhramannatha
                        saghoṣavān |
                        <caesura/>
                         pipāsā vardhate tīvrā bhramo mūrcchā ca jāyate || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.139"><l> uccārito mūtritaś ca na śāntimadhigacchati |
                        <caesura/>
                         viṭśūlametajjānīyādbhiṣak paramadāruṇam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.140"><l> kṣipraṃ doṣaharaṃ kāryaṃ bhiṣajā sādhu jānatā
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         svedanaṃ śamanaṃ caiva nirūhāḥ snehabastayaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.141"><l> pūrvoddiṣṭān pāyayeta yogān koṣṭhaviśodhanān
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         udāvartaharāś cāsya kriyāḥ sarvāḥ sukhāvahāḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.142"><l> atimātraṃ yadā bhuktaṃ pāvake mṛdutāṃ gate |
                        <caesura/>
                         sthirībhūtaṃ tu tatkoṣṭhe vāyurāvṛtya tiṣṭhati || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.143"><l> avipākagataṃ hy annaṃ śūlaṃ tīvraṃ karotyati
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         mūrcchādhmānaṃ vidāhaś ca hṛdutkleśo vilambikā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.144"><l> viricyate chardayati kampate 'tha vimuhyati |
                        <caesura/>
                         avipākād bhavec chūlas tv annadoṣasamudbhavaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.145"><l> vamanaṃ laṅghanaṃ svedaḥ pācanaṃ
                        phalavartayaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         kṣārāś cūrṇāni guṭikāḥ śasyante śūlanāśanāḥ ||
                        <caesura/>
                         gulmāvasthāḥ kriyāḥ kāryā yathāvat sarvaśūlinām ||</l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.trailer"><l>iti suśrutasaṃhitāyām uttaratantrāntargate
                        kāyacikitsātantre gulmapratiṣedho nāma (caturtho 'dhyāyaḥ, āditaḥ)
                        dvicatvāriṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ ||42 ||</l></lg>

                <div n="43" type="adhyāya">
                    <head>tricatvāriṃśattamo 'dhyāyaḥ | </head>
                    <pb facs="811" n="727a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.1"><l> athāto hṛdrogapratiṣedhaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.3"><l> vegāghātoṣṇarūkṣānnairatimātropasevitaiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         viruddhādhyaśanājīrṇairasātmyaiś cāpi(cāti) bhojanaiḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.4"><l> dūṣayitvā rasaṃ doṣā viguṇā hṛdayaṃ gatāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         kurvanti hṛdaye bādhāṃ hṛdrogaṃ taṃ pracakṣate || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.5"><l> caturvidhaḥ sa doṣaiḥ syāt kṛmibhiś ca pṛthak
                        pṛthak |
                        <caesura/>
                         lakṣaṇaṃ tasya vakṣyāmi cikitsitamanantaram || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.6"><l> āyamyate mārutaje hṛdayaṃ tudyate tathā |
                        <caesura/>
                         nirmathyate dīryate ca sphoṭyate pāṭyate 'pi ca || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.7"><l> tṛṣṇoṣādāhacoṣāḥ syuḥ paittike hṛdayaklamaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         dhūmāyanaṃ ca mūrcchā ca svedaḥ śoṣo mukhasya ca || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.8"><l> gauravaṃ kaphasaṃsrāvo 'ruciḥ stambho
                        'gnimārdavam |
                        <caesura/>
                         mādhuryam api cāsyasya balāsāvatate hṛdi || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.9"><l> utkleśaḥ ṣṭhīvanaṃ todaḥ śūlo hṛllāsakastamaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         aruciḥ śyāvnetratvaṃ śoṣaś ca kṛmije bhavet || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.10"><l> bhramaklamau sādaśoṣau jñeyās teṣām upadravāḥ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         kṛmije kṛmijātīnāṃ ślaiṣmikāṇāṃ ca ye matāḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.11"><l> vātopasṛṣṭe hṛdaye vāmayet snigdhamāturam |
                        <caesura/>
                         dvipañcamūlakvāthena sasnehalavaṇena tu || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.12"><l> pippalyelāvacāhiṅguyavabhasmāni saindhavam |
                        <caesura/>
                         sauvarcalamatho śuṇṭhīmajamodāṃ ca cūrṇitam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.13"><l> phaladhānyāmlakaulatthadadhimadyāsavādibhiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         pāyayeta viśuddhaṃ ca snehenānyatamena vā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.14"><l> bhojayejjīrṇaśālyannaṃ jāṅgalaiḥ saghṛtai
                        rasaiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         vātaghnasiddhaṃ tailaṃ ca dadyādbastiṃ pramāṇataḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.15"><l><pb facs="812" n="728a"/>
                        śrīparṇīmadhukakṣaudrasitotpalajalairvamet |
                        <caesura/>
                         pittopasṛṣṭe hṛdaye seveta madhuraiḥ śṛtam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.16"><l> ghṛtaṃ kaṣāyāṃś coddiṣṭān pittajvaravināśanān
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         tṛptasya ca rasairmukhyairmadhuraiḥ saghṛtairbhiṣak || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.17"><l> sakṣaudraṃ vitaredbastau tailaṃ
                        madhukasādhitam |
                        <caesura/>
                         vacānimbakaṣāyābhyāṃ vāntaṃ hṛdi kaphātmake || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.18"><l> cūrṇaṃ tu pāyayetoktaṃ vātaje bhojayec ca tam
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         phalādimatha mustādiṃ triphalāṃ vā piben naraḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.19"><l> śyāmātrivṛtkalkayutaṃ ghṛtaṃ vāpi virecanam |
                        <caesura/>
                         balātailairvidadhyāc ca bastiṃ bastiviśāradaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.20"><l> kṛmihṛdrogiṇaṃ snigdhaṃ bhojayet piśitaudanam
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         dadhnā ca palalopetaṃ tryahaṃ paścād virecayet || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.21"><l> sugandhibhiḥ salavaṇairyogaiḥ sājājiśarkaraiḥ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         viḍaṅgagāḍhaṃ dhānyāmlaṃ pāyayetāpyanantaram || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.22"><l> hṛdayasthāḥ patantyevamadhastāt krimayo nṛṇām
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         yavānnaṃ vitareccāsya saviḍaṅgamataḥ param ||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.trailer"><l>iti suśrutasaṃhitāyām uttaratantrāntargate
                        kāyacikitsātantre hṛdrogapratiṣedho nāma (pañcamo 'dhyāyaḥ,
                        āditaḥ) tricatvāriṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ ||43 ||</l></lg>

                <div n="44" type="adhyāya">
                    <head>catuś catvāriṃśattamo 'dhyāyaḥ | </head>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.44.1"><l> athātaḥ pāṇḍurogapratiṣedhaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.44.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvandariḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.44.3"><l> vyavāyamamlaṃ lavaṇāni madyaṃ mṛdaṃ
                        divāsvapnamatīva tīkṣṇam |
                        <caesura/>
                         niṣevamāṇasya vidūṣya raktaṃ kurvanti doṣāstvaci pāṇḍubhāvam || </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="813" n="729a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.44.4"><l> pāṇḍvāmayo 'ṣṭārdhavidhaḥ pradiṣṭaḥ

                        <caesura/>
                         sarveṣu caiteṣv iha pāṇḍubhāvo yato 'dhiko 'taḥ khalu pāṇḍurogaḥ
                        || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.44.5"><l> tvaksphoṭanaṃ ṣṭhīvanagātrasādau mṛdbhakṣaṇaṃ

                        <caesura/>
                         viṇmūtrapītatvamathāvipāko bhaviṣyatas tasya puraḥsarāṇi || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.44.6"><l> sa kāmalāpānakipāṇḍurogaḥ kumbhāhvayo
                        lāgharako(lāghavako)+alasākhyaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         vibhāṣyate lakṣaṇamasya kṛtsnaṃ nibodha vakṣyāmyanupūrvaśastat || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.44.7"><l> kṛṣṇekṣaṇaṃ kṛṣṇasirāvanaddhaṃ

                        <caesura/>

                        ca || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.44.8"><l> pītekṣaṇaṃ pītasirāvanaddhaṃ

                        ca || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.44.10"><l> sarvātmake sarvam idaṃvyavasyed vakṣyāmi

                        <caesura/>
                         yo hyāmayānte sahasānnamamlamadyādapathyāni ca tasya pittam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.44.11"><l> karoti pāṇḍuṃ vadanaṃ viśeṣāt pūrveritau
                        tandribalakṣayau ca |
                        <caesura/>
                        <pb facs="814" n="730a"/> bhedas tu tasyāḥ khalu kumbhasāhvaḥ    
                    śopho mahāṃs tatra ca parvabhedaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.44.12"><l> jvarāṅgamardabhramasādatandrākṣayānvito

                        <caesura/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.44.13"><l> upadravās teṣv aruciḥ pipāsā chardirjvaro

                        <caesura/>

                        <caesura/>

                <div n="45" type="adhyāya">
                    <head>pañcacatvāriṃśattamo 'dhyāyaḥ | </head>
                    <pb facs="818" n="734a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.1"><l> athāto raktapittapratiṣedhaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.3"><l> krodhaśokabhayāyāsaviruddhān nātapānalān |
                        <caesura/>
                         kaṭvamlalabaṇakṣāratīkṣṇoṣṇātividāhinaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.4"><l> nityam abhyasato duṣṭo rasaḥ pittaṃ prakopayet
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         vidagdhaṃ svaguṇaiḥ pittaṃ vidahatyāśu śoṇitam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.5"><l> tataḥ pravartate raktam ūrdhvaṃ cādho dvidhāpi
                        vā |
                        <caesura/>
                         āmāśayād vrajed ūrdhvam adhaḥ pakvāśayād vrajet || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.6ab"><l> vidagdhayor dvayoś cāpi dvidhā bhāgaṃ
                        pravartate |</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.6cd"><l>kecit sayakṛtaḥ plīhnaḥ pravadanty asṛjo gatim
                        || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.7ab"><l> ūrdhvaṃ sādhyam adho yāpyam asādhyaṃ
                        yugapadgatam |</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.7cd"><l> sadanaṃ śītakāmitvaṃ kaṇṭhadhūmāyanaṃ vamiḥ
                        || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.8"><l> lohagandhiś ca niḥśvāso bhavaty asmin
                        bhaviṣyati |
                        <caesura/>
                         bāhyāsṛglakṣaṇais tasya saṅkhyā doṣocchritīr viduḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.9"><l>
                        daurbalyaśvāsakāsajvaravamathumadāstandritādāhamūrcchā bhukte
                        cānne vidāhas tv adhṛtir api sadā hṛdyatulyā ca pīḍā |
                        <caesura/>
                         tṛṣṇā kaṇṭhasya bhedaḥ śirasi ca davanaṃ pūtiniṣṭhīvanaṃ ca dveṣo
                        bhakte 'vipāko viratir api rate raktapittopasargāḥ || </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="819" n="735a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.10"><l> māṃsaprakṣālanābhaṃ kvathitam iva ca yat
                        kardamāmbhonibhaṃ vā medaḥpūyāsrakalpaṃ yakṛd iva yadi vā
                        pakvajambūphalābham |
                        <caesura/>
                         yat kṛṣṇaṃ yac ca nīlaṃ bhṛśam atikuṇapaṃ yatra coktā vikārās tad
                        varjyaṃ raktapittaṃ surapatidhanuṣā yac ca tulyaṃ vibhāti || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.11"><l> nādau saṃgrāhyam udriktaṃ yad asṛgbalino
                        'śnataḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         tat pāṇḍugrahaṇīkuṣṭhaplīhagulmajvarāvaham || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.12"><l> adhaḥpravṛttaṃ vamanair ūrdhvagaṃ ca
                        virecanaiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         jayed anyatarad vāpi kṣīṇasya śamanair asṛk || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.13"><l> atipravṛddhadoṣasya pūrvaṃ lohitapittinaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         akṣīṇabalamāṃsāgneḥ kartavyamapatarpaṇam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.14"><l> laṅghitasya tataḥ peyāṃ vidadhyāt
                        svalpataṇḍulām |
                        <caesura/>
                         rasayūṣau pradātavyau surabhis nehasaṃskṛtau |
                        <caesura/>
                         tarpaṇaṃ pācanaṃ lehān sarpīṣi vividhāni ca || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.15"><l> drākṣāmadhukakāśmaryasitāyuktaṃ virecanam |
                        <caesura/>
                         yaṣṭīmadhukayuktaṃ ca sakṣaudraṃ vamanaṃ hitam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.16"><l> payāṃsi śītāni rasāś ca jāṅgalāḥ satīnayūṣāś
                        ca saśāliṣaṣṭikāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         paṭolaśelūsuniṣaṇṇayūthikāvaṭātimuktāṅkurasinduvārajam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.17"><l> hitaṃ ca śākaṃ ghṛtasaṃskṛtaṃ sadā tathaiva
                        dhātrīphaladāḍimānvitam |
                        <caesura/>
                         rasāś ca pārāvataśaṅkhakūrmajās tathā yavāgvo vihitā ghṛtottarāḥ
                        || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.18"><l> santānikāś cotpalavargasādhite kṣīre praśastā
                        madhuśarkarottarāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         himāḥ pradehā madhurā gaṇāś ca ye ghṛtāni pathyāni ca
                        raktapittinām || </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="820" n="736a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.19"><l> madhūkaśobhāñjanakovidārajaiḥ priyaṅgukāyāḥ
                        kusumaiś ca cūrṇitaiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         bhiṣag vidadhyāc caturaḥ samākṣikān hitāya lehānasṛjaḥ praśāntaye
                        || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.20"><l> lihyāc ca dūrvāvaṭajāṃś ca pallavān
                        madhudvitīyān sitakarṇikasya ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         hitaṃ ca kharjūraphalaṃ samākṣikaṃ phalāni cānyāny api
                        tadguṇānyatha || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.21ab"><l> raktātisāraproktāṃś ca yogān atrāpi yojayet
                        | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.21cd"><l>śuddhekṣukāṇḍam āpothya nave kumbhe
                        himāmbhasā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.22"><l> yojayitvā kṣiped rātrāvākāśe sotpalaṃ tu tat |
                        <caesura/>
                         prātaḥ srutaṃ kṣaudrayutaṃ pibec choṇitapittavān || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.23"><l> pibec chītakaṣāyaṃ vā jambvāmrārjunasaṃbhavam
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         udumbaraphalaṃ piṣṭvā pibet tad rasam eva vā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.24"><l> trapuṣīmūlakalkaṃ vā sakṣaudraṃ taṇḍulāmbunā |
                        <caesura/>
                         pibed akṣasamaṃ kalkaṃ yaṣṭīmadhukam eva vā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.25"><l> candanaṃ madhukaṃ rodhram evam eva samaṃ pibet
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         karañjabījam evaṃ vā sitākṣaudrayutaṃ pibet || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.26"><l> majjānamiṅgudasyaivaṃ piben madhukasaṃyutam |
                        <caesura/>
                         sukhoṣṇaṃ lavaṇaṃ bījaṃ kārañjaṃ dadhimastunā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.27"><l> pibed vāpi tryahaṃ martyo raktapittābhipīḍitaḥ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         raktapittaharāḥ śastāḥ ṣaḍete yogasattamāḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.28"><l> pathyāś caivāvapīḍeṣu ghrāṇataḥ prasrute 'sṛji
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         atinisrutarakto vā kṣaudrayuktaṃ pibed asṛk |
                        <caesura/>
                         yakṛd vā bhakṣayed ājamāmaṃ pittasamāyutam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.29"><l> palāśavṛkṣasvarase vipakvaṃ
                        <caesura/>
                         sarpiḥ pibet kṣaudrayutaṃ suśītam |
                        <caesura/>
                         vanaspatīnāṃ svarasaiḥ kṛtaṃ vā
                        <caesura/>
                         saśarkaraṃ kṣīraghṛtaṃ pibed vā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.30"><l> drākṣāmuśīrāṇy atha padmakaṃ sitā
                        pṛthakpalāṃśānyudake samāvapet |
                        <caesura/>
                         sthitaṃ niśāṃ tadrudhir āmayaṃ jayet pītaṃ payo vāmbusamaṃ
                        hitāśinaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.31"><l> turaṅgavarcaḥsvarasaṃ samākṣikaṃ pibet
                        sitākṣaudrayutaṃ vṛṣasya vā |
                        <caesura/>
                         lihet tathā vāstukabījacūrṇaṃ kṣaudrānvitaṃ taṇḍulasāhvayaṃ vā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.32"><l> lihyāc ca lājāñjanacūrṇamekamevaṃ
                        sitākṣaudrayutāṃ tugākhyām |
                        <caesura/>
                         drākṣāṃ sitāṃ tiktakarohiṇīṃ ca himāmbunā vā madhukena yuktām || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.33ab"><l> pathyāmahiṃsrāṃ rajanīṃ ghṛtaṃ ca
                        <caesura/>
                         lihyāt tathā śoṇitapittarogī |</l></lg>

                    <pb facs="821" n="737a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.33cd"><l>
                        vāsākaṣāyotpalamṛtpriyaṅgurodhrāñjanāmbhoruhakeśarāṇi || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.34"><l> pītvā sitākṣaudrayutāni jahyāt pittasṛjo
                        vegamudīrṇamāśu |
                        <caesura/>
                         gāyatrijambvarjunakovidāraśirīṣarodhrāśanaśālmalīnām || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.35"><l> puṣpāṇi śigroś ca vicūrṇya leho madhvanvitaḥ
                        śoṇitapittaroge |
                        <caesura/>
                         sakṣaudramindīvarabhasmavāri karañjabījaṃ madhusarpiṣī ca || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.36ab"><l> jambvarjunāmrakvathitaṃ ca toyaṃ ghnanti
                        trayaḥ pittam asṛk ca yogāḥ |</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.36cd"><l> mūlāni puṣpāṇi ca mātuluṅgyāḥ piṣṭvā
                        pibettaṇḍuladhāvanena || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.37"><l> ghrāṇapravṛtte jalamāśu deyaṃ saśarkaraṃ
                        nāsikayā payo vā |
                        <caesura/>
                         drākṣārasaṃ kṣīraghṛtaṃ pibedvā saśarkaraṃ cekṣurasaṃ himaṃ vā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.38"><l> śītopacāraṃ madhuraṃ ca kuryād
                        <caesura/>
                         viśeṣataḥ śoṇitapittaroge |
                        <caesura/>
                         drākṣāghṛtakṣaudrasitāyutena
                        <caesura/>
                         vidārigandhādivipācitena || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.39"><l> kṣīreṇa cāsthāpanam agryam uktaṃ
                        <caesura/>
                         hitaṃ ghṛtaṃ cāpy anuvāsanārtham |
                        <caesura/>
                         priyaṅgurodhrāñjanagairikotpalaiḥ
                        <caesura/>
                         suvarṇakālīyakaraktacandanaiḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.40"><l> sitāśvagandhāmbudayaṣṭikāhvayair
                        <caesura/>
                         mṛṇālasaugandhikatulyapeṣitaiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         nirūhya cainaṃ payasā samākṣikair
                        <caesura/>
                         ghṛtaplutaiḥ śītajalāvasecitam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.41"><l> kṣīraudanaṃ bhuktam athānuvāsayed
                        <caesura/>
                         ghṛtena yaṣṭīmadhusādhitena ca |
                        <caesura/>
                        <pb facs="822" n="738a"/> adhovahaṃ śoṇitameṣa nāśayet
                        <caesura/>
                         tathātisāraṃ rudhirasya dustaram || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.42"><l> virekayoge tvati caiva śasyate
                        <caesura/>
                         vāmyaś ca rakte vijite balānvitaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.43"><l> evaṃvidhā uttarabāstayaś ca mūtrāśayasthe
                        rudhire vidheyāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         pravṛttarakteṣu ca pāyujeṣu kuryād vidhānaṃ khalu raktapaittam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.44"><l> vidhiś cāsṛgdare 'pyeṣa strīṇāṃ kāryo vijānatā
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         śastrakarmaṇi raktaṃ ca yasyātīva pravartate || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.45"><l> trayāṇām api doṣāṇāṃ śoṇite 'pi ca sarvaśaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         liṅgāny ālokya kartavyaṃ cikitsitam anantaram ||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.trailer"><l>iti suśrutasaṃhitāyām uttaratantrāntargate
                        kāyacikitsātantre raktapittapratiṣedho nāma (saptamo 'dhyāyaḥ,

                <div n="46" type="adhyāya">
                    <head>ṣaṭcatvāriṃśattamo 'dhyāyaḥ | </head>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.46.1"><l> athāto mūrcchāpratiṣedhaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.46.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.46.3"><l> kṣīṇasya bahudoṣasya viruddhāhārasevinaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         vegāghātād abhīghātād dhīnasattvasya vā punaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.46.4"><l> karaṇāyataneṣūgrā bāhyeṣv ābhyantareṣu ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         niviśante yadā doṣās tadā mūrcchanti mānavāḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.46.5"><l> hṛtpīḍā jṛmbhaṇaṃ glāniḥ saṃjñānāśo balasya ca
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         sarvāsāṃ pūrvarūpāṇi yathāsvaṃ tā vibhāvayet || </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="823" n="739a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.46.6"><l> saṃjñāvahāsu nāḍīṣu pihitāsvanilādibhiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         tamo 'bhyupaiti sahasā sukhaduḥkhavyapohakṛt || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.46.7"><l> sukhaduḥkhavyapohāc ca naraḥ patati kāṣṭhavat |
                        <caesura/>
                         moho mūrccheti tāṃ prāhuḥ ṣaḍvidhā sā prakīrtitā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.46.8"><l> vātādibhiḥ śoṇitena madyena ca viṣeṇa ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         ṣaṭsvapyetāsu pittaṃ hi prabhutvenāvatiṣṭhate || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.46.9"><l> apasmāroktaliṅgāni tāsām uktāni tattvataḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         pṛthivyambhastamorūpaṃ raktagandhaś ca tanmayaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.46.10"><l> tasmād raktasya gandhena mūrcchanti bhuvi
                        mānavāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         dravyasvabhāva ity eke sthitās tu viṣamadyayoḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.46.11"><l> guṇās tīvrataratvena sthitās tu viṣamadyayoḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         ta eva tasmāj jāyeta mohas tābhyāṃ yatheritaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.46.11ef"><l/></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.46.12"><l> madyena vilapaṃś chete naṣṭavibhrāntamānasaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         gātrāṇi vikṣipan bhūmau jarāṃ yāvan na yāti tat || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.46.13"><l> vepathusvapnatṛṣṇāḥ syuḥ stambhaś ca
                        viṣamūrcchite |
                        <caesura/>
                         veditavyaṃ tīvrataraṃ yathāsvaṃ viṣalakṣaṇaiḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.46.14"><l> sekāvagāhau maṇayaḥ sahārāḥ śītāḥ pradehā
                        vyajanānilāś ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         śītāni pānāni ca gandhavanti sarvāsu mūrcchāsvanivāritāni || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.46.15"><l> sitāpriyālekṣurasaplutāni
                        drākṣāmadhūkasvarasānvitāni |
                        <caesura/>
                         kharjūrakāśmaryarasaiḥ śṛtāni pānāni sarpīṃṣi ca jīvanāni || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.46.16"><l> siddhāni varge madhure payāṃsi sadāḍimā
                        jāṅgalajā rasāś ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         tathā yavā lohitaśālayaś ca mūrcchāsu pathyāś ca sadā satīnāḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.46.17"><l> bhujaṅgapuṣpaṃ maricānyuśīraṃ kolasya madhyaṃ
                        ca pibet samāni |
                        <caesura/>
                         śītena toyena bisaṃ mṛṇālaṃ kṣaudreṇa kṛṣṇāṃ sitayā ca pathyām || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.46.18"><l> kuryāc ca nāsāvadanāvarodhaṃ kṣīraṃ pibed vāpy
                        atha mānuṣīṇām |
                        <caesura/>
                         mūrcchāṃ prasaktāṃ tu śirovirekairjayed abhīkṣṇaṃ vamanaiś ca
                        tīkṣṇaiḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.46.19"><l> harītakīkvāthaśṛtaṃ ghṛtaṃ vā dhātrīphalānāṃ
                        svarasaiḥ kṛtaṃ vā |
                        <caesura/>
                         drākṣāsitādāḍimalājavanti śītāni nīlotpalapadmavanti || </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="825" n="741a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.46.20"><l> pibet kaṣāyāṇi ca gandhavanti pittajvaraṃ yāni
                        śamaṃ nayanti |
                        <caesura/>
                         prabhūtadoṣas tamaso 'tirekāt saṃmūrcchito naiva vibudhyate yaḥ
                        || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.46.21"><l> saṃnyastasaṃjño bhṛśaduś cikitsyo jñeyas tadā
                        buddhimatā manuṣyāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         yathā maloṣṭaṃ salile niṣiktaṃ samuddhered āśvavilīnam eva || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.46.21a"><l/></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.46.22ab"><l> tadvac cikitset tvarayā bhiṣak tam asvedanaṃ
                        mṛtyuvaśaṃ prayātam | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.46.22cd"><l> tīkṣṇāñjanābhyañjanadhūmayogais tathā
                        nakhābhyantarato trapātaiḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.46.23ab"><l> vāditragītānunayair apūrvair vighaṭṭanair
                        guptaphalāvagharṣaiḥ | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.46.23cd"><l> ābhiḥ kriyābhiś ca na labdhasaṃjñaḥ
                        sānāhalālāśvasanaś ca varjyaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.46.23ef"><l/></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.46.24ab"><l> prabuddhasaṃjñaṃ vamanānulomyaistīkṣṇair
                        viśuddhaṃ laghupathyabhuktam | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.46.24cdef"><l> phalatrikaiś citrakanāgarāḍhyais
                        tathāśmajātāj jatunaḥ prayogaiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         saśarkarair māsam upakrameta viśeṣato jīrṇaghṛtaṃ sa pāyyaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.46.25"><l> yathāsvaṃ ca jvaraghnāni kaṣāyāṇyupayojayet |
                        <caesura/>
                         sarvamūrcchāparītānāṃ viṣajāyāṃ viṣāpaham ||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.46.trailer"><l>iti suśrutasaṃhitāyām uttaratantrāntargate
                        kāyacikitsātantre mūrcchāpratiṣedho nāma (aṣṭamo 'dhyāyaḥ, āditaḥ)
                        ṣaṭcatvāriṃśattamo 'dhyāyaḥ ||46 ||</l></lg>
                </div>

                <div n="47" type="adhyāya">
                    <head>saptacatvāriṃśattamo 'dhyāyaḥ | </head>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.47.1"><l> athātaḥ pānātyayapratiṣedhaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.47.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.47.3"><l> madyamuṣṇaṃ tathā tīkṣṇaṃ sūkṣmaṃ viśadam eva
                        ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         rūkṣamāśukaraṃ caiva vyavāyi ca vikāśi ca || </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="826" n="742a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.47.4"><l> auṣṇyācchītopacāraṃ tattaikṣṇyāddhanti
                        manogatim |
                        <caesura/>
                         viśatyavayavān saukṣmyādvaiśadyātkaphaśukranut || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.47.5"><l> mārutaṃ kopayed raukṣyādāśutvāccāśukarmakṛt |
                        <caesura/>
                         harṣadaṃ ca vyavāyitvād vikāśitvād visarpati || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.47.6"><l> tadamlaṃ rasataḥ proktaṃ laghu rocanadīpanam |
                        <caesura/>
                         kecillavaṇavarjyāṃs tu rasānatrādiśanti hi || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.47.7"><l> snigdhais tadannairmāṃsaiś ca bhakṣyaiś ca saha
                        sevitam |
                        <caesura/>
                         bhavedāyuḥprakarṣāya balāyopacayāya ca || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.47.8"><l> kāmyatā manasastuṣṭirdhairyaṃ tejo 'tivikramaḥ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         vidhivat sevyamāne tu madye sannihitā guṇāḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.47.9"><l> tadevānannamajñena sevyamānamamātrayā |
                        <caesura/>
                         kayāgninā hy agnisamaṃ sametya kurute madam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.47.10"><l> madena karaṇānāṃ tu bhāvānyatve kṛte sati |
                        <caesura/>
                         nigūḍham api bhāvaṃ svaṃ prakāśīkurute 'vaśaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.47.11"><l> tryavasthaś ca mado jñeyaḥ pūrvo madhyo 'tha
                        paś cimaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         pūrve vīryaratiprītiharṣabhāṣyādivardhanam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.47.12"><l> pralāpo madhyame moho yuktāyuktakriyās tathā |
                        <caesura/>
                         visaṃjñaḥ paś cime śete naṣṭakarmakriyāguṇaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.47.13"><l> ślaiṣmikānalpapittāṃś ca
                        snigdhānmātropasevinaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         pānaṃ na bādhate 'tyarthaṃ viparītāṃs tu bādhate || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.47.14"><l> nirbhaktamekāntata eva madyaṃ niṣevyamāṇaṃ
                        manujena nityam |
                        <caesura/>
                         utpādayet kaṣṭatamān vikārānāpādayec cāpi śarīrabhedam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.47.15"><l> kruddhena bhītena pipāsitena śokābhitaptena
                        bubhukṣitena |
                        <caesura/>
                         vyāyāmabhārādhvaparikṣatena vegāvarodhābhihatena cāpi || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.47.16"><l> atyamlabhakṣyāvatatodareṇa sājīrṇabhuktena
                        tathābalena |
                        <caesura/>
                        <pb facs="827" n="743a"/> uṣṇābhitaptena ca sevyamānaṃ karoti                         madyaṃ vividhān vikārān || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.47.17"><l> pānātyayaṃ paramadaṃ pānājīrṇam athāpi vā |
                        <caesura/>
                         pānavibhramamugraṃ ca teṣāṃ vakṣyāmi lakṣaṇam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.47.18"><l> stambhāṅgamardhṛdayagrahatodakampāḥ pānātyaye
                        'nilakṛte śiraso rujaś ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         svedapralāpamukhaśoṣaṇadāhamūrcchāḥ pittātmake vadanalocanapītatā
                        ca || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.47.19"><l> śleṣmātmake vamathuśītakaphaprasekāḥ
                        sarvātmake bhavati sarvavikārasaṃpat |
                        <caesura/>
                         ūṣmāṇamaṅgagurutāṃ virasānanatvaṃ śleṣmādhikatvamaruciṃ
                        malamūtrasaṅgam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.47.20"><l> liṅgaṃ parasya tu madasya vadanti
                        tajjñāstṛṣṇāṃ rujāṃ śirasi sandhiṣu cāpi bhedam |
                        <caesura/>
                         ādhmānamudgiraṇamamlaraso vidāho 'jīrṇasya pānajanitasya vadanti
                        liṅgam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.47.21"><l> jñeyāni tatra bhiṣajā suviniś citāni
                        pittaprakopajanitāni ca kāraṇāni |
                        <caesura/>
                         hṛdgātratodavamathujvarakaṇṭhadhūmamūrcchākaphasravaṇamūrdharujo
                        vidāhaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.47.22"><l> dveṣaḥ surān navikṛteṣu ca teṣu teṣu taṃ
                        pānavibhramam uśanty akhilena dhīrāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         hīnottarauṣṭham atiśītam amandadāhaṃ tailaprabhāsyam atipānahataṃ
                        (tailaprabhāsyam apipānahataṃ) vijahyāt || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.47.23"><l> jihvauṣṭhadantam asitaṃ tv athavāpi nīlaṃ pīte
                        ca yasya nayane rudhiraprame ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         hikkājvarau vamathuvepathupārśvaśūlāḥ kāsabhramāvapi ca pānahataṃ
                        bhajante || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.47.24"><l> teṣāṃ nivāraṇam idaṃhi mayocyamānaṃ
                        vyaktābhidhānamakhilena vidhiṃ nibodha |
                        <caesura/>
                         madyaṃ tu cukramaricārdrakadīpyakuṣṭhasauvarcalāyutamalaṃ
                        pavanasya śāntyai || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.47.25"><l> pṛthvīkadīpyakamahauṣadhahiṅgubhir vā
                        sauvarcalena ca yutaṃ vitaret sukhāya |
                        <caesura/>
                         āmrātakāmraphaladāḍimamātuluṅgaiḥ kuryāc chubhāny api ca
                        ṣaḍavapānakāni || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.47.26"><l> seveta vā phalarasopahitān
                        rasādīnānūpavargapiśitāny api gandhavanti |
                        <caesura/>
                         pittātmake madhuravargakaṣāyamiśraṃ madyaṃ hitaṃ
                        samadhuśarkaramiṣṭagandham || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.47.27"><l> pītvā ca madyam api cekṣurasapragāḍhaṃ
                        niḥśeṣataḥ kṣaṇamavasthitamullikhec ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         lāvaiṇatittirirasāṃś ca pibedanamlān maudgān sukhāya saghṛtān
                        sasitāṃś ca yūṣān || </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="828" n="744a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.47.28"><l> pānātyaye kaphakṛte kaphamullikhec ca madyena
                        bimbividulodakasaṃyutena |
                        <caesura/>
                         seveta tiktakaṭukāṃś ca rasānudārān yūṣāṃś ca tiktakaṭukopahitān
                        hitāya || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.47.29"><l> pathyaṃ yavānnavikṛtāni ca jāṅgalāni
                        śleṣmaghnamanyadapi yac ca niratyayaṃ syāt |
                        <caesura/>
                         kuryāc ca sarvamatha sarvabhave vidhānaṃ ddvandvodbhave
                        dvayamavekṣya yathāpradhānam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.47.30"><l> sāmānyamanyadapi yac ca samagramagryaṃ

                        <caesura/>
                         tvaṅgāgapuṣpamagadhailamadhūkadhānyaiḥ ślakṣṇairajājimaricaiś ca
                        kṛtaṃ samāṃśaiḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.47.31"><l> pānaṃ kapittharasavāriparūṣakāḍhyaṃ

                        <caesura/>

                        karavīrajalodbhavaiś ca || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.47.32"><l> piṣṭaiḥ sapadmakayutair api sārivādyaiḥ sekaṃ
                        jalaiś ca vitaredamalaiḥ suśītaiḥ |
                        <caesura/>

                        upetam || </l></lg>

                        <caesura/>

                        trapuṣībhavaṃ yat || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.47.34"><l> kārpāsinīmatha ca nāgabalāṃ ca tulyāṃ pītvā

                        <caesura/>

                        kāyacikitsātantre madātyayapratiṣedho nāma (navamo 'dhyāyaḥ,
                        āditaḥ) saptacatvāriṃśattamo 'dhyāyaḥ ||47 ||</l></lg>
