                <div n="48" type="adhyāya">
                    <head>aṣṭacatvāriṃśattamo 'dhyāyaḥ | </head>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.1"><l> athātas tṛṣṇāpratiṣedham adhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ
                        || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.3"><l> satataṃ yaḥ pibedvāri na tṛptimadhigacchati |
                        <caesura/>
                         punaḥ kāṅkṣati toyaṃ ca taṃ tṛṣṇārditam ādiśet || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.4"><l>
                        sakṣobhaśokaśramamadyapānādrūkṣāmlaśuṣkoṣṇakaṭūpayogāt |
                        <caesura/>
                         dhātukṣayāllaṅghanasūryatāpāt pittaṃ ca vātaś ca bhṛśaṃ
                        pravṛddhau || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.5"><l> srotāṃsi saṃdūṣayataḥ sametau yānyambuvāhīni
                        śarīriṇāṃ hi |
                        <caesura/>
                         srotaḥsvapāṃvāhiṣu dūṣiteṣu jāyeta tṛṣṇātibalā tatas tu || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.6"><l> tisraḥ smṛtāstāḥ kṣatajā caturthī kṣayāt
                        tathānyām asamudbhavā ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         syāt saptamī bhaktanimittajā tu nibodha liṅgāny anupūrvaśas tu || </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="835" n="751a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.7"><l> tālvoṣṭhakaṇṭhāsyaviśoṣadāhāḥ
                        saṃtāpamohabhramavipralāpāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         pūrvāṇi rūpāṇi bhavanti tāsām utpattikāleṣu viśeṣatas tu || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.8"><l> śuṣkāsyatā mārutasaṃbhavāyāṃ todas tathā
                        śaṅkhaśiraḥsu cāpi |
                        <caesura/>
                         srotonirodho virasaṃ ca vaktraṃ śītabhir adbhiś ca vivṛddhim eti
                        || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.9"><l> mūrcchāpralāpārucivaktraśoṣāḥ pītekṣaṇatvaṃ
                        pratataś ca dāhaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         śītābhikāṅkṣā mukhatiktatā ca pittātmikāyāṃ paridhūpanaṃ ca || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.10"><l> kaphāvṛtābhyāmanilānalābhyāṃ kapho 'pi śuṣkaḥ
                        prakaroti tṛṣṇām |
                        <caesura/>
                         nidrā gurutvaṃ madhurāsyatā ca tayārditaḥ śuṣyati cātimātram || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.11"><l> kaṇṭhopalepo mukhapicchilatvaṃ śītajvaraś
                        chardirarocakaś ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         kaphātmikāyāṃ gurugātratā ca śākhāsu śophastvavipāka eva |
                        <caesura/>
                         etāni rūpāṇi bhavanti tasyāṃ tayārditaḥ kāṅkṣati nāti cāmbhaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.12"><l> kṣatasya rukśoṇitanirgamābhyāṃ tṛṣṇā caturthī
                        kṣatajā matā tu |
                        <caesura/>
                         tayābhibhūtasya niśādināni gacchanti duḥkhaṃ pibato 'pi toyam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.13"><l> rasakṣayādyā kṣayajā matā sā tayārditaḥ
                        śuṣyati dahyate ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         atyarthamākāṅkṣati cāpi toyaṃ tāṃ sannipātād itikecidāhuḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.14"><l> rasakṣayoktāni ca lakṣaṇāni tasyāmaśeṣeṇa
                        bhiṣagvyavasyet |
                        <caesura/>
                         tridoṣaliṅgāmasamudbhavā ca hṛcchūlaniṣṭhīvanasādayuktā || </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="836" n="752a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.15"><l> snigdhaṃ tathāmlaṃ lavaṇaṃ ca bhuktaṃ
                        gurvannamevātitṛṣāṃ karoti |
                        <caesura/>
                         kṣīṇaṃ vicittaṃ badhiraṃ tṛṣārtaṃ vivarjayennirgatajihvamāśu || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.16"><l> tṛṣṇābhivṛddhāvudare ca pūrṇe taṃ
                        vāmayenmāgadhikodakena |
                        <caesura/>
                         vilobhanaṃ cātra hitaṃ vidheyaṃ syāddāḍimāmrātakamātuluṅgaiḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.17"><l> tisraḥ prayogairiha sannivāryāḥ śītaiś ca
                        samyag rasavīryajātaiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         gaṇḍūṣamamlairvirase ca vaktre kuryāc chubhairāmalakasya cūrṇaiḥ
                        || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.18"><l> suvarṇarūpyādibhir agnitaptairloṣṭaiḥ kṛtaṃ vā
                        sikatādibhir vā |
                        <caesura/>
                         jalaṃ sukhoṣṇaṃ śamayet tu tṛṣṇāṃ saśarkaraṃ kṣaudrayutaṃ himaṃ
                        vā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.19"><l> pañcāṅgikāḥ pañcagaṇā ya uktāsteṣv ambu
                        siddhaṃ prathame gaṇe vā |
                        <caesura/>
                         pibet sukhoṣṇaṃ manujo 'cireṇa tṛṣo vimucyeta hi vātajāyāḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.20"><l> pittaghnavargais tu kṛtaḥ kaṣāyaḥ saśarkaraḥ
                        kṣaudrayutaḥ suśītaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         pītastṛṣāṃ pittakṛtāṃ nihanti kṣīraṃ śṛtaṃ vāpy atha jīvanīyaiḥ
                        || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.21"><l> bilvāḍhakīkanyakapañcamūlīdarbheṣu siddhaṃ
                        kaphajāṃ nihanti |
                        <caesura/>
                         hitaṃ bhavec chardanam eva cātra taptena nimbaprasavodakena || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.22"><l> sarvāsu tṛṣṇāsvathavāpi paittaṃ kuryād vidhiṃ
                        tena hi tā na santi |
                        <caesura/>
                         paryāgatodumbarajo rasas tu saśarkaratatkvathitodakaṃ vā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.23"><l> vargasya siddhasya ca sārivādeḥ pātavyamambhaḥ
                        śiśiraṃ tṛṣārtaiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                        <pb facs="837" n="752a"/> kaśeruśṛṅgāṭakapadmamocabisekṣusiddhaṃ                         vāṃ nihanti || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.24"><l> lājotpalośīrakucandanāni dattvā pravāte niśi
                        vāsayet tu |
                        <caesura/>
                         taduttamaṃ toyamudāragandhi sitāyutaṃ kṣaudrayutaṃ vadanti || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.25"><l> drākṣāpragāḍhaṃ ca hitāya vaidyas
                        tṛṣṇārditebhyo vitaren narebhyaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         sasārivādau tṛṇapañcamūle tathotpalādau prathame gaṇe ca || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.26"><l> kuryāt kaṣāyaṃ ca yatheritena madhūkapuṣpādiṣu
                        cāpareṣu |
                        <caesura/>
                         rājādanakṣīrikapītaneṣu ṣaṭ pānakānyatra hitāni ca syuḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.27"><l> satuṇḍikerāṇyathavā pibet tu piṣṭāni
                        kārpāsasamudbhavāni |
                        <caesura/>
                         kṣatodbhavāṃ rugvinivāraṇena jayed rasānāmasṛjaś ca pānaiḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.28"><l> kṣayotthitāṃ kṣīraghṛtaṃ nihanyānmāṃsodakaṃ vā
                        madhukodakaṃ vā |
                        <caesura/>
                         āmodbhavāṃ bilvavacāyutais tu jayet kaṣāyairatha dīpanīyaiḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.29"><l> āmrātabhallātabalāyutāni pibet kaṣāyāṇyatha
                        dīpanāni |
                        <caesura/>
                        <pb facs="838" n="754a"/> gurvannajātāṃ vamanairjayec ca kṣayādṛte                         sarvakṛtāṃ ca tṛṣṇām || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.30"><l> śramodbhavāṃ māṃsaraso nihanti guḍodakaṃ vāpy
                        athavāpi manthaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         bhaktoparodhāttṛṣito yavāgūmuṣṇāṃ piben manthamatho himaṃ ca || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.31"><l> yā snehapītasya bhavec ca tṛṣṇā
                        tatroṣṇamambhaḥ prapiben manuṣyaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         madyodbhavāmardhajalaṃ nihanti madyaṃ tṛṣāṃ yāpi ca madyapasya || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.32"><l> tṛṣṇodbhavāṃ hanti jalaṃ suśītaṃ saśarkaraṃ
                        sekṣurasaṃ tathāmbhaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         svaiḥ svaiḥ kaṣāyairvamanāni tāsāṃ tathā jvaroktāni ca pācanāni
                        || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.33"><l> lepāvagāhau pariṣecanāni kuryāt tathā
                        śītagṛhāṇi cāpi |
                        <caesura/>
                         saṃśodhanaṃ kṣīrarasau ghṛtāni sarvāsu lehānmadhurān himāṃś ca
                        ||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.trailer"><l>iti suśrutasaṃhitāyām uttaratantrāntargate
                        kāyacikitsātantre (daśamo 'dhyāyaḥ āditaḥ) aṣṭacatvāriṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ
                        ||48 ||</l></lg>
                </div>

                <div n="49" type="adhyāya">
                    <head>ekonapañcāśattamo 'dhyāyaḥ | </head>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.1"><l> athātaś chardipratiṣedham adhyāyaṃ
                        vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.3"><l> atidravairatisnigdhairahṛdyairlavaṇairati |
                        <caesura/>
                         akāle cātimātraiś ca tathāsātmyaiś ca bhojanaiḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.4"><l> śramāt kṣayāt tathodvegād ajīrṇāt kṛmidoṣataḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         nāryāś cāpannasattvāyās tathātidrutam aśnataḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.5"><l> atyantāmaparītasya chardervai saṃbhavo dhruvam
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         (bībhatsairhetubhiś cānyairdrutamutkleśito balāt ||) </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="839" n="755a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.6"><l> chādayannānanaṃ vegairardayannaṅgabhañjanaiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         nirucyate chardir iti doṣo vaktrād viniścaran || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.7"><l> doṣānudīrayan vṛddhānudāno vyānasaṅgataḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         ūrdhvamāgacchati bhṛśaṃ viruddhāhārasevanāt || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.8"><l> praseko hṛdayotkleśo bhaktasyānabhinandanam |
                        <caesura/>
                         pūrvarūpaṃ mataṃ chardyā yathāsvaṃ ca vibhāvayet || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.9"><l> pracchardayet phenilamalpamalpaṃ śūlārdito
                        'bhyarditapārśvapṛṣṭhaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         śrāntaḥ saghoṣaṃ bahuśaḥ kaṣāyaṃ jīrṇe 'dhikaṃ sānilajā vamis tu
                        || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.10"><l> yo 'mlaṃ bhṛśaṃ vā kaṭutiktavaktraḥ pītaṃ
                        saraktaṃ haritaṃ vamedvā |
                        <caesura/>
                         sadāhacoṣajvaravaktraśoṣo mūrcchānvitaḥ pittanimittajā sā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.11"><l> yo hṛṣṭaromā madhuraṃ prabhūtaṃ śuklaṃ himaṃ
                        sāndrakaphānuviddham |
                        <caesura/>
                         abhaktaruggauravasādayukto vamedvamī sā kaphakopajā syāt || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.12"><l> sarvāṇi rūpāṇi bhavanti yasyāṃ sā
                        sarvadoṣaprabhavā matā tu |
                        <caesura/>
                         bībhatsajā dauhṛdajāmajā ca sātmyaprakopāt kṛmijā ca yā hi |
                        <caesura/>
                         sā pañcamī tāṃ ca vibhāvayet tu doṣocchrayeṇaiva yathoktamādau || </l></lg>

                      <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.13"><l> śūlahṛllāsabahulā kṛmijā ca viśeṣataḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         kṛmihṛdrogatulyena lakṣaṇena ca lakṣitā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.14"><l>                         <pb facs="840" n="756a"/> kṣīṇasyopadravairyuktāṃ sāsṛkpūyāṃ 
                       sacandrikām |
                        <caesura/>
                         chardiṃ prasaktāṃ kuśalo nārabheta cikitsitum || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.15"><l> āmāśayotkleśabhavā hi sarvāstasmād dhitaṃ
                        laṅghanam eva tāsu || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.16"><l> vamīṣu bahudoṣāsu chardanaṃ hitam ucyate |
                        <caesura/>
                         virecanaṃ vā kurvīta yathādoṣocchrayaṃ bhiṣak || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.17"><l> saṃsargaś cānupūrveṇa yathāsvaṃ bheṣajāyutaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         laghūni pariśuṣkāṇi sātmyānyannāni cācaret || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.18"><l> yathāsvaṃ ca kaṣāyāṇi jvaraghnāni prayojayet |
                        <caesura/>
                         hanyāt kṣīraghṛtaṃ pītaṃ chardiṃ pavanasaṃbhavām || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.19"><l> sasaindhavaṃ pibet sarpirvātacchardinivāraṇam
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         mudgāmalakayūṣo vā sasarpiṣkaḥ sasaindhavaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         yavāgūṃ madhumiśrāṃ vā pañcamūlīkṛtāṃ pibet || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.20"><l> pibedvā vyaktasindhūtthaṃ phalāmlaṃ vaiṣkiraṃ
                        rasam |
                        <caesura/>
                         sukhoṣṇalavaṇaṃ cātra hitaṃ snehavirecanam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.21"><l> pittopaśamanīyāni pākyāni śiśirāṇi ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         kaṣāyāṇyupayuktāni ghnanti pittakṛtāṃ vamīm || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.22"><l> śodhanaṃ madhuraiś cātra drākṣārasasam āyutaiḥ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         balavatyāṃ praśaṃsanti sarpis tailvakam eva ca || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.23"><l> āragvadhādiniryūhaṃ daśāṅgayogam eva vā |
                        <caesura/>
                         pāyayetātha sakṣaudraṃ kaphajāyāṃ cikitsakaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="841" n="757a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.24"><l> kṛtaṃ guḍūcyā vidhivat kaṣāyaṃ himasaṃjñitam |
                        <caesura/>
                         tisṛṣv api bhavet pathyaṃ mākṣikeṇa samanvitam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.25"><l> bībhatsajāṃ hṛdyatamairdauhṛdīṃ kāṅkṣitaiḥ
                        phalaiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         laṅghanairvamanaiś cāmāṃ sātmyaiḥ sātmyaprakopajām || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.26"><l> kṛmihṛdrogavac cāpi kṛmijāṃ sādhayed vamīm |
                        <caesura/>
                         vitarec ca yathādoṣaṃ śastaṃ vidhimanantaram || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.27"><l> dadhittharasasṃsaktāṃ pippalīṃ mākṣikānvitām |
                        <caesura/>
                         muhurmuhurnaro līḍhvā chardibhyaḥ pravimucyate(pratimucyate) || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.28"><l> samākṣikā madhurasā pītā vā taṇḍulāmbunā |
                        <caesura/>
                         tarpaṇaṃ vā madhuyutaṃ tisṛṇām api bheṣajam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.29"><l> svayaṅguptāṃ sayaṣṭyāhvāṃ
                        taṇḍulāmbumadhudravām |
                        <caesura/>
                         pibed yavāgūm athavā siddhāṃ patraiḥ karañjajaiḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.30"><l> yuktāmlalavaṇāḥ piṣṭāḥ kustumburyo 'thavā
                        hitāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         taṇḍulāmbuyutaṃ khādet kapitthaṃ tryūṣaṇena vā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.31"><l> sitācandanamadhvāktaṃ lihyād vā makṣikāśakṛt |
                        <caesura/>
                         pibet payo 'gnitaptaṃ ca nirvāpya gṛhagodhikām || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.32"><l> sarpiḥkṣaudrayutān vāpi lājaśaktūn pibet tathā
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         sarpiḥkṣaudrasitopetāṃ māgadhīṃ vā lihet tathā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.33"><l> dhātrīrase candanaṃ vā ghṛṣṭaṃ mudgadalāmbunā
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         kolāmalakamajjānaṃ lihyādvā trisugandhikam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.34"><l> sakṣaudrāṃ śālilājānāṃ yavagūṃ vā piben naraḥ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         ghreyāṇyupaharec cāpi manoghrāṇasukhāni ca || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.35"><l> jāṅgalāni ca māṃsāni śubhāni pānakāni ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         bhojanāni vicitrāṇi kuryāt sarvāsvatandritaḥ ||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.trailer"><l>iti suśrutasṃhitāyām uttaratantrāntargate
                        kāyacikitsātantre chardipratiṣedho nāma (ekādaśo 'dhyāyaḥ, āditaḥ)
                        ekonapañcāśattamo 'dhyāyaḥ ||49 ||</l></lg>

                <div n="50" type="adhyāya">
                    <head>pañcāśattamo 'dhyāyaḥ | </head>
                    <pb facs="842" n="758a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.50.1"><l> athāto hikkāpratiṣedhaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.50.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.50.3"><l> vidāhiguruviṣṭambhir ūkṣābhiṣyandibhojanaiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         śītapānāsanasthānarajodhūmānilānalaiḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.50.4"><l> vyāyāmakarmabhārādhvavegāghātāpatarpaṇaiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         āmadoṣābhighātastrīkṣayadoṣaprapīḍanaiḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.50.5"><l> viṣamāśanādhyanaśanais tathā samaśanair api |
                        <caesura/>
                         hikkā śvāsaś ca kāsaś ca nṛṇāṃ samupajāyate || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.50.6"><l> muhurmuhurvāyurudeti sasvano yakṛtplihāntrāṇi
                        mukhādivākṣipan |
                        <caesura/>
                         sa ghoṣavānāśu hinastyasūn yatastatas tu hikketi bhiṣagbhir
                        ucyate || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.50.7"><l> annajāṃ yamalāṃ kṣudrāṃ gambhīrāṃ mahatīṃ tathā
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         vāyuḥ kaphenānugataḥ pañca hikkāḥ karoti hi || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.50.8"><l> mukhaṃ kaṣāyamaratirgauravaṃ kaṇṭhavakṣasoḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         pūrvarūpāṇi hikkānāmāṭopo jaṭharasya ca || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.50.9"><l> tvaramāṇasya cāhāraṃ bhuñjānasyāthavā ghanam |
                        <caesura/>
                         vāyurannairavastīrṇaḥ kaṭukairardito bhṛśam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.50.10"><l> hikkayatyūrdhvago bhūtvā tāṃ vidyādannajāṃ
                        bhiṣak |
                        <caesura/>
                         cireṇa yamalairvegairyā hikkā saṃpravartate || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.50.11"><l> kampayantī śirogrīvaṃ yamalāṃ tāṃ vinirdiśet |
                        <caesura/>
                         vikṛṣṭakālairyā vegairmandaiḥ samabhivartate || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.50.12"><l> kṣudrikā nāma sā hikkā jatrumūlāt pradhāvitā |
                        <caesura/>
                         nābhipravṛttā yā hikkā ghorā gambhīranādinī || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.50.13"><l> śuṣkauṣṭhakaṇṭhajihvāsyaśvāsapārśvarujākarī |
                        <caesura/>
                         anekopadravayutā gambhīrā nāma sā smṛtā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.50.14"><l> marmāṇyāpīḍayantīva satataṃ yā pravartate |
                        <caesura/>
                         dehamāyamya vegena ghoṣayatyatitṛṣyataḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         mahāhikketi sā jñeyā sarvagātraprakampinī || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.50.15"><l> āyamyate hikkato 'ṅgāni yasya dṛṣṭiś cordhvaṃ
                        tāmyate yasya gāḍham |
                        <caesura/>
                         kṣīṇo 'nnadviṭ kāsate yaś ca hikkī tau dvāvantyau varjayed
                        dhikkamānau || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.50.16"><l> prāṇāyāmodvejanatrāsanāni sūcītodaiḥ
                        saṃbhramaś cātra śastaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         yaṣṭyāhvaṃ vā mākṣikeṇāvapīḍe pippalyo vā śarkarācūrṇayuktāḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.50.17"><l> sarpiḥ koṣṇaṃ kṣīramikṣo raso vā nātikṣīṇe
                        chardanaṃ śāntihetoḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         nārīpayaḥpiṣṭamaśuklacandanaṃ ghṛtaṃ sukhoṣṇaṃ ca sasaindhavaṃ
                        tathā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.50.18"><l> cūrṇīkṛtaṃ saindhavamambhasāthavā nihanti
                        hikkāṃ ca hitaṃ ca nasyataḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                        <pb facs="844" n="760a"/> yuñjyāddhūmaṃ śālaniryāsajātaṃ naipālaṃ                         vā goviṣāṇodbhavaṃ vā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.50.19"><l> sarpiḥsnigdhaiś carmabālaiḥ kṛtaṃ vā
                        hikkāsthāne svedanaṃ cāpi kāryam |
                        <caesura/>
                         kṣaudropetaṃ gairikaṃ kāñcanāhvaṃ lihyādbhasma
                        grāmyasattvāsthijaṃ vā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.50.20"><l> tadvacchvāvinmeṣagośalyakānāṃ(gośallakānāṃ)
                        romāṇyantardhūmadagdhāni cātra |
                        <caesura/>
                         madhvājyāktaṃ barhipatraprasūtamevaṃ bhasmaudumbaraṃ tailvakaṃ vā
                        || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.50.21"><l> svarjikṣāraṃ bījapūrādrasena kṣaudropetaṃ
                        hanti līḍhvāśu hikkām |
                        <caesura/>
                         sarpiḥsnigdhā ghnanti hikkāṃ yavāgvaḥ koṣṇagrāsāḥ pāyaso vā
                        sukhoṣṇaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.50.22"><l> śuṇṭhītoye sādhitaṃ kṣīramājaṃ tadvat pītaṃ
                        śarkarāsaṃyutaṃ vā |
                        <caesura/>
                         ātṛptervā sevyamānaṃ nihantyād ghrātaṃ hikkāmāśu mūtraṃ tvajāvyoḥ
                        || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.50.23"><l> sapūtikīṭaṃ laśunogragandhāhiṅgvabjamācūrṇya
                        subhāvitaṃ tat |
                        <caesura/>
                         kṣaudraṃ sitāṃ vāraṇakeśaraṃ ca pibedrasenekṣumadhūkajena || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.50.24"><l> pibet palaṃ vā lavaṇottamasya dvābhyāṃ
                        palābhyāṃ haviṣaḥ samagram |
                        <caesura/>
                         harītakīṃ koṣṇajalānupānāṃ pibed ghṛtaṃ kṣāramadhūpapannam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.50.25"><l> rasaṃ kapitthānmadhupippalībhyāṃ śuktipramāṇaṃ
                        prapibet sukhāya || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.50.26"><l> kṛṣṇāṃ sitāṃ cāmalakaṃ ca līḍhaṃ saśṛṅgaveraṃ
                        madhunāthavāpi |
                        <caesura/>
                         kolāsthimajjāñjanalājacūrṇaṃ hikkā nihanyānmadhunāvalīḍham || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.50.27"><l><pb facs="845" n="761a"/> pāṭalāyāḥ phalaṃ                         puṣpaṃ gairikaṃ kaṭurohiṇī |
                        <caesura/>
                         kharjūramadhyaṃ māgadhyaḥ kāśīśaṃ dadhināma ca || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.50.28"><l> catvāra ete yogāḥ syuḥ pratipādapradarśitāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         madhudvitīyāḥ kartavyāste hikkāsu vijānatā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.50.29"><l>
                        kapotapārāvatalāvaśaka(lla)śvadaṃṣṭragodhāvṛṣadaṃśajān rasān |
                        <caesura/>
                         pibet phalāmlānahimān sasaindhavān snigdhāṃs tathaiva
                        rṣyamṛgadvijodbhavān || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.50.30"><l> virecanaṃ pathyatamaṃ sasaindhavaṃ ghṛtaṃ
                        sukhoṣṇaṃ ca sitopalāyutam |
                        <caesura/>
                         sadāgatāvūrdhvagate 'nuvāsanaṃ vadanti kecic ca hitāya hikkinām
                        ||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.50.trailer"><l>iti suśrutasaṃhitāyām uttaratantrāntargate
                        kāyacikitsātantre hikkāpratiṣedho nāma (dvādaśo 'dhyāyaḥ, āditaḥ)
                        pañcāśattamo 'dhyāyaḥ ||50 ||</l></lg>
                </div>

                <div n="51" type="adhyāya">
                    <head>ekapañcāśattamo 'dhyāyaḥ | </head>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.1"><l> athātaḥ śvāsapratiṣedhaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.3"><l> yair eva kāraṇairhikkā bahubhiḥ saṃpravartate |
                        <caesura/>
                         tair eva kāraṇaiḥ śvāso ghoro bhavati dehinām || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.4"><l> vihāya prakṛtiṃ vāyuḥ prāṇo 'tha kaphasaṃyutaḥ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         śvāsayatyūrdhvago bhūtvā taṃ śvāsaṃ paricakṣate || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.5"><l> kṣudrakastamakaś chinno mahānūrdhvaś ca
                        pañcadhā |
                        <caesura/>
                         bhidyate sa mahāvyādhiḥ śvāsa eko viśeṣataḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.6"><l> prāgrūpaṃ tasya hṛtpīḍā bhaktadveṣo 'ratiḥ parā
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         ānāhaḥ pārśvayoḥ śūlaṃ vairasyaṃ vadanasya ca || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.7"><l> kiñcidārabhamāṇasya yasya śvāsaḥ pravartate |
                        <caesura/>
                         niṣaṇṇasyaiti śāntiṃ ca sa kṣudra iti saṃjñitaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="846" n="762a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.8"><l> tṛṭsvedavamathuprāyaḥ kaṇṭhaghurghurikānvitaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         viśeṣāddurdine tāmyec chvāsaḥ sa tamako mataḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.9"><l> ghoṣeṇa mahatāviṣṭaḥ sakāsaḥ sakapho naraḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         yaḥ śvasity abalo 'nnadviṭ suptas tamakapīḍitaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.10"><l> sa śāmyati kaphe hīne svapataś ca vidhardhate
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         mūrcchājvarābhibhūtasya jñeyaḥ pratamakas tu saḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.11"><l> ādhmāto dahyamānena bastinā sarujaṃ naraḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         sarvaprāṇena vicchinnaṃ śvasyācchinnaṃ tam ādiśet || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.12"><l> niḥsaṃjñaḥ pārśvaśūlārtaḥ śuṣkakaṇṭho
                        'tighoṣavān |
                        <caesura/>
                         saṃrabdhanetrastvāyamya yaḥ śvasyāt sa mahān smṛtaḥ | </l></lg>
                    <l/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.13"><l> marmasv āyamyamāneṣu śvasan mūḍho muhuś ca yaḥ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         ūrdhvaprekṣī hataravas tam ūrdhvaśvāsam ādiśet || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.14"><l> kṣudraḥ sādhyatamas teṣāṃ tamakaḥ kṛcchra
                        ucyate |
                        <caesura/>
                         trayaḥ śvāsā na sidhyanti tamako durbalasya ca || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.15"><l> snehabastiṃ vinā kecidūrdhvaṃ cādhaś ca
                        śodhanam |
                        <caesura/>
                         mṛdu rāṇavatāṃ śreṣṭhaṃ śvāsināmādiśanti hi || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.16"><l> śvāse kāse ca hikkāyāṃ hṛdroge cāpi pūjitam |
                        <caesura/>
                         ghṛtaṃ purāṇaṃ saṃsiddhamabhayāviḍarāmaṭhaiḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.17"><l> sauvarcalābhayābilvaiḥ saṃskṛtaṃ vānavaṃ
                        ghṛtam |
                        <caesura/>
                         pippalyādipratīvāpaṃ siddhaṃ vā prathame gaṇe || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.18"><l> sapañcalavaṇaṃ sarpiḥ śvāsakāsau vyapohati |
                        <caesura/>
                         hiṃsrāviḍaṅgapūtīkatriphalāvyoṣacitrakaiḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.19"><l> dvikṣīraṃ sādhitaṃ sarpiś caturguṇajalāplutam
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         kolamātraiḥ pibettaddhi śvāsakāsau vyapohati || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.20"><l> arśāṃsyarocakaṃ gulmaṃ śakṛdbhedaṃ kṣayaṃ
                        tathā |
                        <caesura/>
                         kṛtsne vṛṣakaṣāye vā pacet sarpiś caturguṇe || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.21"><l> tanmūlakusumāvāpaṃ śītaṃ kṣaudreṇa yojayet |
                        <caesura/>
                         śṛṅgīmadhūlikābhārgīśuṇṭhītārkṣyasitāmbudaiḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.22"><l> saharidraiḥ sayaṣṭyāhvaiḥ samairāvāpya yogataḥ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         ghṛtaprasthaṃ paceddhīmān śītatoye caturguṇe || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.23"><l> śvāsaṃ kāsaṃ tathā hikkāṃ sarpiretanniyacchati
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                        <pb facs="847" n="763a"/> suvahā kālikā bhargī śukākhyā naiculaṃ                         phalam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.24"><l> kākādanī śṛṅgaveraṃ varṣabhūrbṛhatīdvayam |
                        <caesura/>
                         kolamātrairghṛtaprasthaṃ pacedebhir jaladvikam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.25"><l> kaṭūṣṇaṃ pītam etad dhi śvāsāmayavināśanam |
                        <caesura/>
                         sauvarcalayavakṣārakaṭukāvyoṣacitrakaiḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.26"><l> vacābhayāviḍaṅgaiś ca sādhitaṃ śvāsaśāntaye |
                        <caesura/>
                         gopavally udake siddhaṃ syād anyad dviguṇe ghṛtam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.27"><l> pañcaitāni havīṃṣyāhurbhiṣajaḥ śvāsakāsayoḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         tālīśatāmalakyugrājīvantīkuṣṭhasaindhavaiḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.28"><l> bilvapuṣkarabhūtīkasauvarcalakaṇāgnibhiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         pathyā tejovatī yuktaiḥ sarpirjalacaturguṇam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.29"><l> hiṅgupādayutaṃ siddhaṃ sarvaśvāsaharaṃ param |
                        <caesura/>
                         vāsāghṛtaṃ ṣaṭpalaṃ vā ghṛtaṃ cātra hitaṃ bhavet || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.30"><l> tailaṃ daśaguṇe siddhaṃ bhṛṅgarājarase śubhe |
                        <caesura/>
                         sevyamānaṃ yathānyāyaṃ śvāsakāsau vyapohati || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.31"><l> phalāmlā viṣkirarasāḥ snigdhāḥ
                        pravyaktasaindhavāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         eṇādīnāṃ śirobhir vā kaulatthā vā susaṃskṛtāḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.32"><l> hanyuḥ śvāsaṃ ca kāsaṃ ca saṃskṛtāni payāṃsi
                        ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         timirasya ca bījāni karkaṭākhyā ca cūrṇitā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.33"><l> durālabhātha pippalyaḥ kaṭukākhyā harītakī |
                        <caesura/>
                         śvāvinmayūraromāṇi kolā māgadhikākaṇāḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.34"><l> bhārgītvak śṛṅgaveraṃ ca śarkarā śallakāṅgajam
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         nṛttakauṇḍakabījāni cūrṇitāni tu kevalam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.35"><l> pañca ślokārdhikāstvete lehā ye samyag īritāḥ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         sarpirmadhubhyāṃ te lehyāḥ kāsaśvāsārditairnaraiḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.36"><l> saptacchadasya puṣpāṇi pippalīś cāpi mastunā |
                        <caesura/>
                         pibet saṃcūrṇya madhunā dhānāś cāpy atha bhakṣayet || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.37"><l> arkāṅkurairbhāvitānāṃ yavānāṃ sādhvanekaśaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         tarpaṇaṃ vā pibedeṣāṃ sakṣaudraṃ śvāsapīḍitaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.38"><l> śirīṣakadalīkundapuṣpaṃ māgadhikāyutam |
                        <caesura/>
                         taṇḍulāmbuyutaṃ pītvā jayec chvāsānaśeṣataḥ || </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="848" n="764a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.39"><l> kolamajjāṃ tālamūlamṛṣyacarmamasīm api |
                        <caesura/>
                         lihyāt kṣaudreṇa bhārgīṃ vā sarpirmadhusamāyutām || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.40"><l> nīcaiḥkadambabījaṃ vā sakṣaudraṃ taṇḍulāmbunā
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         drākṣāṃ harītakīṃ kṛṣṇāṃ karkaṭākhyāṃ durālabhām || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.41"><l> sarpirmadhubhyāṃ vilihan hanti śvāsān
                        sudāruṇān |
                        <caesura/>
                         haridrāṃ maricaṃ drākṣāṃ guḍaṃ rāsnāṃ kaṇāṃ śaṭīm || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.42"><l> lihyāttailena tulyāni śvāsārto hitabhojanaḥ |
                        <caesura/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.43"><l> lihyācchvāsseṣu kāseṣu vājināṃ vā śakṛdrasam |
                        <caesura/>
                         pāṇḍurogeṣu śotheṣu ye yogāḥ saṃprakīrtitāḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.44"><l> śvāsakāsāpahāste 'pi kāsaghnā ye ca kīrtitāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         bhārgītvak tryūṣaṇaṃ tailaṃ haridrāṃ kaṭurohiṇīm || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.45"><l> pippalīṃ maricaṃ caṇḍāṃ gośakṛdrasam eva ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         talakoṭasya bījeṣu pacedutkārikāṃ śubhām || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.46"><l> sevyamānā nihantyeṣā śvāsānāśu sudustarān |
                        <caesura/>
                         purāṇasarpiḥ pippalyaḥ kaulatthā jāṅgalā rasāḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.47"><l> surā sauvīrakaṃ hiṅgu mātuluṅgaraso madhu |
                        <caesura/>
                         drākṣāmalakabilvāni śastāni śvāsihikkinām || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.48"><l> śvāsahikkāparigataṃ snigdhaiḥ svedair upācaret
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         āktaṃ lavaṇatailābhyāṃ tairasya grathitaḥ kaphaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.49"><l> khastho vilayanaṃ yāti mārutaś ca praśāmyati |
                        <caesura/>
                         svinnaṃ jñātvā tataś caiva bhojayitvā rasaudanam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.50"><l> vātaśleṣmavibandhe vā bhiṣag dhūmaṃ prayojayet
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         manaḥśilādevadāruharidrācchadanāmiṣaiḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.51"><l> lākṣorubūkamūlaiś ca kṛtvā vartīr vidhānataḥ |
                        <caesura/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.52"><l> śṛṅgabālakhurasnāyutvak samastaṃ gavām api |
                        <caesura/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.53"><l> ete sarve sasarpiṣkā dhūmāḥ kāryā vijānatā |
                        <caesura/>
                         balīyasi kaphagraste vamanaṃ savirecanam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.54"><l> durbale caiva rūkṣe ca tarpaṇaṃ hitam ucyate |
                        <caesura/>
                         jāṅgalorabhrajairmāṃsairānūpair vā susaṃskṛtaiḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.55"><l> nidigdhikāṃ cāmalakapramāṇāṃ hiṅgvardhayuktāṃ
                        madhunā suyuktām |
                        <caesura/>
                         lihan naraḥ śvāsanipīḍito hi śvāsaṃ jayaty eva balāttryaheṇa || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.51.56"><l> yathāgniriddhaḥ pavanānuviddho vajraṃ yathā vā
                        surarājamuktam |
                        <caesura/>
                         rogāstathaite khalu durnivārāḥ śvāsaś ca kāsaś ca vilambikā ca
                        ||</l></lg>

                <div n="52" type="adhyāya">
                    <head>dvipañcāśattamo 'dhyāyaḥ | </head>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.52.1"><l> athātaḥ kāsapratiṣedham adhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ
                        || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.52.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.52.3"><l> uktā ye hetavo nṝṇāṃ rogayoḥ śvāsahikkayoḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         kāsasyāpi ca vijñeyāsta evotpattihetavaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.52.4"><l> dhūmopaghātādrajasas tathaiva
                        vyāyāmarūkṣānnaniṣevaṇāc ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         vimārgagatvād api bhojanasya vegāvarodhāt kṣavathos tathaiva || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.52.5"><l> prāṇo hyudānānugataḥ praduṣṭaḥ
                        saṃbhinnakāṃsyasvanatulyaghoṣaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         nireti vaktrāt sahasā sadoṣaḥ kāsaḥ sa vidvadbhir udāhṛtas tu || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.52.6"><l> sa vātapittaprabhavaḥ kaphāc ca kṣatāttathānyaḥ
                        kṣayajo 'paraś ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         pañcaprakāraḥ kathito bhiṣagbhir vivardhito yakṣmavikārakṛt syāt
                        || </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="850" n="766a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.52.7"><l> bhaviṣyatas tasya tu kaṇṭhakaṇḍūrbhojyoparodho
                        galatālulepaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         svaśabdavaiṣamyamarocako 'gnisādaś ca liṅgāni bhavantyamūni || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.52.8"><l> hṛcchaṅkhamūrdhodarapārśvaśūlī kṣāmānanaḥ
                        kṣīṇabalasvaraujāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         prasaktamantaḥkaphamīraṇena kāset tu śuṣkaṃ svarabhedayuktaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.52.9"><l>
                        urovidāhajvaravaktraśoṣairabhyarditastiktamukhastṛṣārtaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         pittena pītāni vamet kaṭūni kāset sa pāṇḍuḥ paridahyamānaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.52.10"><l> pralipyamānena(vilipyamānena) mukhena sīdan
                        śirorujārtaḥ kaphapūrṇadehaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         abhaktaruggauravasādayuktaḥ kāseta nā sāndrakaphaṃ kaphena || </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="850" n="766b"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.52.11"><l> vakṣo 'timātraṃ vihataṃ tu yasya
                        vyāyāmabhārādhyayanābhighātaiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         viśliṣṭavakṣāḥ sa naraḥ saraktaṃ ṣṭhīvatyabhīkṣṇaṃ kṣatajaṃ
                        tamūhuḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.52.12"><l> sa gātraśūlajvaradāhamohān prāṇakṣayaṃ
                        copalabheta kāsī |
                        <caesura/>
                         śuṣyan viniṣṭhīvati durbalas tu prakṣīṇamāṃso rudhiraṃ sapūyam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.52.13"><l> sasarvaliṅgaṃ bhṛśaduś cikitsyaṃ cikitsitajñāḥ
                        kṣayajaṃ vadanti |
                        <caesura/>
                         vṛddhatvamāsādya bhavet tu yo vai yāpyaṃ tamāhurbhiṣajas tu kāsam
                        || </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="851" n="767a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.52.14"><l>
                        śṛṅgīvacākaṭphalakattṛṇābdadhānyābhayābhārgyamarāhvaviśvam |

                         uṣṇāmbunā hiṅguyutaṃ tu pītvā baddhāsyamapyāśu jahāti kāsam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.52.15"><l>
                        phalatrikavyoṣaviḍaṅgaśṛṅgīrāsnāvacāpadmakadevakāṣṭhaiḥ |
                        <caesura/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.52.16"><l> pathyāṃ sitāmāmalakāni lājāṃ samāgadhīṃ cāpi
                        vicūrṇya śuṇṭhīm |
                        <caesura/>
                         sarpirmadhubhyāṃ vilihīta kāsī sasaindhavāṃ voṣṇajalena kṛṣṇām || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.52.17"><l> khādedguḍaṃ nāgarapippalībhyāṃ drākṣāṃ ca
                        sarpirmadhunāvalihyāt |
                        <caesura/>

                        || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.52.18"><l> lihyād ghṛtakṣaudrayutāṃ samāṃśāṃ sitopalāṃ vā

                        <caesura/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.52.20"><l> bastasya mūtreṇa sukhāmbunā vā dantīṃ

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.52.22"><l> dhūme praśastā ghṛtasaṃprayuktā

                <div n="53" type="adhyāya">

                    <head>tripañcāśattamo 'dhyāyaḥ | </head>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.53.1"><l> athātaḥ svarabhedapratiṣedham adhyāyaṃ
                        vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.53.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.53.3"><l> atyuc cabhāṣaṇaviṣādhyayanātigītaśītādibhiḥ
                        prakupitāḥ pavanādayas tu |
                        <caesura/>
                         srotaḥsu te svaravaheṣu gatāḥ pratiṣṭhāṃ hanyuḥ svaraṃ bhavati
                        cāpi hi ṣaḍvidhaḥ saḥ || </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="855" n="771a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.53.4"><l> vātena kṛṣṇanayanānanamūtravarcā bhinnaṃ śanair
                        vadati gadgadavat svaraṃ ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         pittena pītavadanākṣipurīṣamūtro brūyād galena
                        paridāhasamanvitena || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.53.5"><l> kṛcchrāt kaphena satataṃ kapharuddhakaṇṭho
                        mandaṃ śanairvadati cāpi divā viśeṣaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         sarvātmake bhavati sarvavikārasaṃpadavyaktatā ca
                        vacasastamasādhyam āhuḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.53.6"><l> dhūpyeta vāk kṣayakṛte kṣayamāpnuyāc ca vāgeṣa
                        cāpi(vāpi) hatavāk parivarjanīyaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         antargalaṃ svaramalakṣyapadaṃ cireṇa medaś cayādvadati
                        digdhagalauṣṭhatāluḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.53.7"><l> kṣīṇasya vṛddhasya kṛśasya cāpi cirotthito yaś
                        ca sahopajātaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         medasvinaḥ sarvasamudbhavaś ca svarāmayo yo na sa siddhimeti || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.53.8"><l> snigdhān svarāturanarānapakṛṣṭadoṣān nyāyena
                        tān vamanarecanabastibhiś ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         nasyāvapīḍamukhadhāvanadhūmalehaiḥ saṃpādayec ca vividhaiḥ
                        kavalagrahaiś ca || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.53.9ab"><l> yaḥ śvāsakāsavidhir ādita eva coktas taṃ cāpy
                        aśeṣam avatārayituṃ yateta | </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="856" n="772a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.53.9cd"><l> vaiśeṣikaṃ ca vidhim ūrdhvam ato vadāmi taṃ
                        vai svarāturahitaṃ nikhilaṃ nibodha || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.53.10"><l> svaropaghāte 'nilaje bhuktopari ghṛtaṃ pibet |
                        <caesura/>
                         kāsamardakavātārkamārkavasvarase śṛtam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.53.11"><l> pītaṃ ghṛtaṃ hanty anilaṃ siddham ārtagale
                        rase |
                        <caesura/>
                         yavakṣārājamodābhyāṃ citrakāmalakeṣu vā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.53.12"><l> devadārvagnikābhyāṃ vā siddhāmājaṃ samākṣikam
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         sukhodakānupāno vā sasarpiṣko guḍaudanaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.53.13"><l> kṣīrānupānaṃ pitte tu pibet sarpiratandritaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         aśnīyāc ca sasarpiṣkaṃ yaṣṭīmadhukapāyasam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.53.14"><l> lihyānmadhurakāṇāṃ vā cūrṇaṃ madhughṛtāplutam
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         śatāvarīcūrṇayogaṃ balācūrṇam athāpi vā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.53.15"><l> pibet kaṭūni mūtreṇa kaphaje svarasaṃkṣaye |
                        <caesura/>
                         lihyādvā madhutailābhyāṃ bhuktvā khādet kaṭūni vā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.53.16"><l> svaropaghāte medoje kaphavadvidhiriṣyate |
                        <caesura/>
                         sarvaje kṣayaje cāpi pratyākhyāyācaret kriyām || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.53.16a"><l/></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.53.17"><l> śarkarāmadhumiśrāṇi śṛtāni madhuraiḥ saha |
                        <caesura/>
                         pibet payāṃsi yasyoc cairvadato 'bhihataḥ svaraḥ ||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.53.trailer"><l>iti suśrutasaṃhitāyām uttaratantrāntargate
                        kāyacikitsātantre chardipratiṣedho nāma (pañcadaśo 'dhyāyaḥ,
                        āditaḥ) tripañcāśattamo 'dhyāyaḥ ||53 ||</l></lg>
                </div>

                <div n="24" type="adhyāya">
                    <head>caturviṃśatitamo 'dhyāyaḥ | </head>
                     <pb facs="735" n="651a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.24.1"><l> athātaḥ pratiśyāyapratiṣedhaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ
                        ||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.24.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ ||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.24.3"><l> nārīprasaṅgaḥ śiraso 'bhitāpo dhūmo rajaḥ
                        śītamatipratāpaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         saṃdhāraṇaṃ mūtrapurīṣayoś ca sadyaḥ pratiśyāyanidānamuktam || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.24.4"><l> cayaṃ gatā mūrdhani mārutādayaḥ pṛthak samastāś
                        ca tathaiva śoṇitam |
                        <caesura/>
                         prakopyamāṇā vividhaiḥ prakopaṇairnṛṇāṃ pratiśyāyakarā bhavanti
                        hi || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.24.5"><l> śirogurutvaṃ kṣavathoḥ pravartanaṃ
                        tathāṅgamardaḥ parihṛṣṭaromatā |
                        <caesura/>
                         upadravāś cāpy apare pṛthagvidhā nṛṇāṃ pratiśyāyapuraḥsarāḥ
                        smṛtāḥ || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.24.6"><l> ānaddhā pihitā nāsā tanusrāvapravartinī |
                        <caesura/>
                         galatālvoṣṭhaśoṣaś ca nistodaḥ śaṅkhayos tathā || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.24.7"><l> svaropaghātaś ca bhavet pratiśyāye 'nilātmake |
                        <caesura/>
                         uṣṇaḥ sapītakaḥ srāvo ghrāṇāt sravati paittike || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.24.8"><l> kṛśo 'tipāṇḍuḥ santapto
                        bhavettṛṣṇānipīḍitaḥ(tṛṣṇābhipīḍitaḥ) |
                        <caesura/>
                         sadhūmaṃ sahasā vahniṃ vamatīva ca mānavaḥ || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.24.9"><l> kaphaḥ kaphakṛte ghrāṇācchuklaḥ śītaḥ
                        sravenmuhuḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         śuklāvabhāsaḥ śūnākṣo bhavedguruśiromukhaḥ || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.24.10"><l> śirogalauṣṭhatālūnāṃ kaṇḍūyanamatīva ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         bhūtvā bhūtvā pratiśyāyo yo 'kasmādvinivartate || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.24.11"><l> saṃpakvo vāpyapakvo vā sa sarvaprabhavaḥ
                        smṛtaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         liṅgāni caiva sarveṣāṃ pīnasānāṃ ca sarvaje || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.24.12"><l><pb facs="736" n="652a"/> raktaje tu pratiśyāye                         raktāsrāvaḥ pravartate |
                        <caesura/>
                         tāmrākṣaś ca bhavejjantururoghātaprapīḍitaḥ || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.24.13"><l> durgandhocchvāsavadanastathā gandhān na vetti
                        ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         mūrcchanti cātra kṛmayaḥ śvetāḥ snigdhāstathāṇavaḥ || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.24.14"><l> kṛmimūrdhavikāreṇa samānaṃ cāsya lakṣaṇam |
                        <caesura/>
                         praklidyati punarnāsā puanaś ca pariśuṣyati || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.24.15"><l> muhurānahyate cāpi muhurvivriyate tathā |
                        <caesura/>
                         niḥśvāsocchvāsadaurgandhyaṃ tathā gandhān na vetti ca || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.24.16"><l> evaṃ duṣṭapratiśyāyaṃ jānīyāt kṛcchrasādhanam
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         sarva eva pratiśyāyā narasyāpratikāriṇaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.24.16-1"><l/></lg>

                    <head>pañcapañcāśattamo 'dhyāyaḥ | </head>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.1"><l> athāta udāvartapratiṣedham adhyāyaṃ
                        vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.3"><l> adhaś cordhvaṃ ca bhāvānāṃ pravṛttānāṃ
                        svabhāvataḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         na vegān dhārayet prājño vātādīnāṃ jijīviṣuḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.4"><l> vātaviṇmūtrajṛmbhāśrukṣavodgāravamīndriyaiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         vyāhanyamānair uditair udāvarto nirucyate || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.5"><l> kṣuttṛṣṇāśvāsanidrāṇām udāvarto vidhāraṇāt |
                        <caesura/>
                         tasyābhidhāsye vyāsena lakṣaṇaṃ ca cikitsitam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.6"><l> trayodaśavidhaś cāsau bhinna etais tu kāraṇaiḥ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         apathyabhojanāc cāpi vakṣyate ca tathāparaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.7"><l> ādhmānaśūlau hṛdayoparodhaṃ śirorujaṃ
                        śvāsamatīva hikkām |
                        <caesura/>
                         kāsapratiśyāyagalagrahāṃś ca balāsapittaprasaraṃ ca ghoram || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.8"><l> kuryād apāno 'bhihataḥ svamārge hanyāt purīṣaṃ
                        mukhataḥ kṣiped vā |
                        <caesura/>
                        <pb facs="861" n="777a"/> āṭopaśūlau parikartanaṃ ca saṅgaḥ                         purīṣasya tathordhvavātaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.9"><l> purīṣamāsyād api vā nireti purīṣavege 'bhihate
                        narasya |
                        <caesura/>
                         mūtrasya vege 'bhihate naras tu kṛcchreṇa mūtraṃ kurute 'lpam
                        alpam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.10"><l> meḍhre gude vaṅkṣaṇabastimuṣkanābhipradeśeṣv

                        <caesura/>
                         ānaddhabastiś ca bhavanti tīvrāḥ śūlāś ca śūlair iva bhinnamūrteḥ

                        <caesura/>

                        <caesura/>
                         śirogurutvaṃ nayanāmayāś ca bhavanti tīvrāḥ saha pīnasena || </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="861" n="777b"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.13"><l> bhavanti gāḍhaṃ kṣavathor vighātāc
                        chirokṣināsāśravaṇeṣu rogāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         kaṇṭhāsyapūrṇatvam atīva todaḥ kūjaś ca vāyor uta vā pravṛttiḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.14"><l> udgāravege 'bhihate bhavanti ghorā vikārāḥ
                        pavanaprasūtāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         charder vighātena bhavec ca kuṣṭhaṃ yenaiva doṣeṇa vidagdham
                        annam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.15"><l> mūtrāśaye pāyuni muṣkayoś ca śopho rujo
                        mūtravinigrahaś ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         śukrāśmarī tatsravaṇaṃ bhaved vā te te vikārā vihate tu śukre || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.16"><l> tandrāṅgamardārucivibhramāḥ syuḥ kṣudho
                        'bhighātāt kṛśatā ca dṛṣṭeḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         kaṇṭhāsyaśoṣaḥ śravaṇāvarodhastṛṣṇābhighātāddhṛdaye vyathā ca || </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="862" n="778a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.17"><l> śrāntasya niḥśvāsavinigraheṇa hṛdrogamohāv
                        athavāpi gulmaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         jṛmbhāṅgamardo 'ṅgaśirokṣijāḍyaṃ nidrābhighātād athavāpi tandrā
                        || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.18"><l> tṛṣṇārditaṃ parikliṣṭaṃ kṣīṇaṃ śūlair
                        abhidrutam |
                        <caesura/>
                         śakṛdvamantaṃ matimān udāvartinam utsṛjet || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.19"><l> sarveṣv eteṣu vidhivad udāvarteṣu kṛtsnaśaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         vāyoḥ kriyā vidhātavyāḥ svamārgapratipattaye || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.20"><l> sāmānyataḥ pṛthaktvena kriyāṃ bhūyo nibodha me
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         āsthāpanaṃ mārutaje snigdhasvinne viśiṣyate || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.21"><l> purīṣaje tu kartavyo vidhir ānāhiko bhavet |
                        <caesura/>
                         sauvarcalāḍhyāṃ madirāṃ mūtre tv abhihate pibet || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.22"><l> elāṃ vāpy atha madyena kṣīraṃ vāpi piben naraḥ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         dhātrīphalānāṃ svarasaṃ sajalaṃ vā pibet tryaham || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.23"><l> rasamaśvapurīṣasya gardabhasyāthavā pibet |
                        <caesura/>
                         māṃsopadaṃśaṃ madhu vā pibedvā sīdhu gauḍikam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.24"><l> bhadradāru ghanaṃ mūrvā haridrā madhukaṃ tathā
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         kolapramāṇāni pibedāntarikṣeṇa vāriṇā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.25"><l> duḥsparśāsvarasaṃ vāpi kaṣāyaṃ kuṅkumasya ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         ervārubījaṃ toyena pibed vā lavaṇīkṛtam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.26"><l> pañcamūlīśṛtaṃ kṣīraṃ drākṣārasam athāpi vā |
                        <caesura/>
                         yogāṃś ca vitared atra pūrvoktān aśmarībhidaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.27"><l> mūtrakṛcchrakramaṃ cāpi kuryān niravaśeṣataḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         bhūyo vakṣyāmi yogān yān mūtrāghātopaśāntaye || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.28"><l> snehaiḥ svedair udāvartaṃ jṛmbhājaṃ
                        samupācaret |
                        <caesura/>
                         aśrumokṣo 'śruje kāryaḥ snigdhasvinnasya dehinaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.29"><l> tīkṣṇāñjanāvapīḍābhyāṃ
                        tīkṣṇagandhopaśiṅghanaiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         vartiprayogair athavā kṣavasaktiṃ pravartayet || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.30"><l> (tīkṣṇaiṣadhapradhamanair athavād ity
                        araśmibhiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                        <pb facs="863" n="779a"/> ) udgāraje kramopetaṃ snaihikaṃ dhūmam                         ācaret || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.31"><l> surāṃ sauvarcalavatīṃ bījapūrarasānvitām |
                        <caesura/>
                         chardyāghātaṃ yathādoṣaṃ samyak snehādibhir jayet || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.32"><l> sakṣāralavaṇopetamabhyaṅgaṃ cātra dāpayet |
                        <caesura/>
                         bastiśuddhikarāvāpaṃ caturguṇajalaṃ payaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.33"><l> āvārināśāt kvathitaṃ pītavantaṃ prakāmataḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         ramayeyuḥ priyā nāryaḥ śukrodāvartinaṃ naram || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.34"><l> kṣudvighāte hitaṃ snigdham uṣṇam alpaṃ ca
                        bhojanam |
                        <caesura/>
                         tṛṣṇāghāte piben manthaṃ yavāgūṃ vāpi śītalām || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.35"><l> bhojyo rasena viśrāntaḥ śramaśvāsāturo naraḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         nidrāghāte pibet kṣīraṃ svapyācceṣṭakathā naraḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.36"><l> ādhmānādyeṣu rogeṣu yathāsvaṃ prayateta hi |
                        <caesura/>
                         yac ca yatra bhavet prāptaṃ tac ca tasmin prayojayet || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.37"><l> vāyuḥ koṣṭhānugo rūkṣaiḥ kaṣāyakaṭutiktakaiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         bhojanaiḥ kupitaḥ sadya udāvartaṃ karoti hi || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.38"><l> vātamūtrapurīṣāsṛkkaphamedovahāni vai |
                        <caesura/>
                         srotāṃsy udāvartayati purīṣaṃ cātivartayet || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.39"><l> tato hṛdbastiśūlārto gauravārucipīḍitaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         vātamūtrapurīṣāṇi kṛcchreṇa kurute naraḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.40"><l> śvāsakāsapratiśyāyadāhamohavamijvarān |
                        <caesura/>
                         tṛṣṇāhikkāśirorogamanaḥśravaṇavibhramān || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.41"><l> labhate ca bahūn anyān vikārān vātakopajān |
                        <caesura/>
                         taṃ tailalavaṇābhyaktaṃ snigdhaṃ svinnaṃ nirūhayet || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.42"><l> doṣato bhinnavarcaskaṃ bhuktaṃ cāpy anuvāsayet
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         na cec chāntiṃ vrajaty evam udāvartaḥ sudāruṇaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.43"><l> athainaṃ bahuśaḥ svinnaṃ yuñjyāt
                        snehavirecanaiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                        <pb facs="864" n="780a"/> pāyayeta trivṛtpīluyavānīramlapācanaiḥ                         || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.44"><l> hiṅgukuṣṭhavacāsvarjiviḍaṅgaṃ vā dviruttaram |
                        <caesura/>
                         yogāv etāv udāvartaṃ śūlaṃ cānilajaṃ hataḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.45"><l> devadārvagnikau kuṣṭhaṃ śuṇṭhīṃ pathyāṃ
                        palaṅkaṣām |
                        <caesura/>
                         pauṣkarāṇi ca mūlāni toyasyardhāḍhake pacet || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.46"><l> pādāvaśiṣṭaṃ tat pītam udāvartam apohati |
                        <caesura/>
                         mūlakaṃ śuṣkam ārdraṃ ca varṣābhūḥ pañcamūlakam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.47"><l> ārevataphalaṃ cāpsu paktvā tena ghṛtaṃ pacet |
                        <caesura/>
                         tat pīyamānaṃ śāsty ugram udāvartam aśeṣataḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.48"><l> vacām ativiṣāṃ kuṣṭhaṃ yavakṣāraṃ harītakīm |
                        <caesura/>
                         kṛṣṇāṃ nirdahanīṃ cāpi pibed uṣṇena vāriṇā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.49"><l> ikṣvākumūlaṃ madanaṃ viśalyātiviṣe vacām |
                        <caesura/>
                         kuṣṭhaṃ kiṇvāgnikau caiva pibet tulyāni pūrvavat || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.50"><l> mūtreṇa devadārvagnitriphalābṛhatīḥ pibet |
                        <caesura/>
                         yavaprasthaṃ phalaiḥ sārdhaṃ kaṇṭakāryā jalāḍhake || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.51"><l> paktvārdhaprasthaśeṣaṃ tu pibed
                        dhiṅgusamanvitam |
                        <caesura/>
                         madanālābubījāni pippalīṃ sanidigdhikām || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.52"><l> saṃcūrṇya pradhamennāḍyā viśaty etad yathā
                        gudam |
                        <caesura/>
                         cūrṇaṃ nikumbhakampillaśyāmekṣvākvagnikodbhavam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.53"><l> kṛtavedhanamāgadhyor lavaṇānāṃ ca sādhayet |
                        <caesura/>
                         gavāṃ mūtreṇa tā vartīḥ kārayet tu gudānugāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         sadyaḥ śarmakarāv etau yogāv amṛtasaṃmatau ||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.55.trailer"><l>iti suśrutasaṃhitāyām uttaratantrāntargate
                        kāyacikitsātantre udāvartapratiṣedho nāma (saptadaśo+ādhyāyaḥ,
                        āditaḥ) pañcapañcāśattamo 'dhyāyaḥ ||55 ||</l></lg>

                <div n="56" type="adhyāya">
                    <head>ṣaṭpañcāśattamo 'dhyāyaḥ | </head>
                    <pb facs="865" n="781a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.56.1"><l> athāto visūcikāpratiṣedham adhyāyaṃ
                        vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.56.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.56.3"><l> ajīrṇam āmaṃ viṣṭabdhaṃ vidagdhaṃ ca yad īritam
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         visūcyalasakau tasmād bhavec cāpi vilambikā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.56.4"><l> sūcībhir iva gātrāṇi tudan saṃtiṣṭhate 'nilaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         yasyājīrṇena sā vaidyair ucyate ti visūcikā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.56.5"><l> na tāṃ parimitāhārā labhante viditāgamāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         mūḍhās tām ajitātmāno labhante kaluṣāśayāḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.56.6"><l> mūrcchātisārau vamathuḥ pipāsā śūlaṃ
                        bhramodveṣṭanajṛmbhadāhāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         vaivarṇyakampau hṛdaye rujaś ca bhavanti tasyāṃ śirasaś ca bhedaḥ
                        || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.56.7"><l> kukṣir ānahyate 'tyarthaṃ pratāmyati vikūjati |
                        <caesura/>
                         niruddho mārutaś cāpi kukṣau viparidhāvati || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.56.8"><l> vātavarconirodhaś ca kukṣau yasya bhṛśaṃ bhavet
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         tasyālasakam ācaṣṭe tṛṣṇodgārāv arodhakau || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.56.9"><l> duṣṭaṃ tu bhuktaṃ kaphamārutābhyāṃ pravartate
                        nordhvam adhaś ca yasya |
                        <caesura/>
                         vilambikāṃ tāṃ bhṛśaduścikitsyām ācakṣate śāstravidaḥ purāṇāḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.56.10"><l> yatrasthamāmaṃ virujet tam eva deśaṃ viśeṣeṇa
                        vikārajātaiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         doṣeṇa yenāvatataṃ svaliṅgais taṃ lakṣayed āmasamudbhavaiś ca || </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="866" n="782a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.56.11"><l> yaḥ śyāvadantauṣṭhanakho 'lpasaṃjñaś
                        chardyardito 'bhyantarayātanetraḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         kṣāmasvaraḥ sarvavimuktasandhir yāyān naraḥ so 'punar āgamāya || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.56.12"><l> sādhyāsu pārṣṇyor dahanaṃ praśastam
                        agnipratāpo vamanaṃ ca tīkṣṇam |
                        <caesura/>
                         pakve tato 'nne tu vilaṅghanaṃ syāt saṃpācanaṃ cāpi virecanaṃ ca
                        || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.56.13"><l> viśuddhadehasya hi sadya eva mūrcchātisārādir
                        upaiti śāntim |
                        <caesura/>
                         āsthāpanaṃ cāpi vadanti pathyaṃ sarvāsu yogānaparān nibodha || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.56.14"><l> pathyāvacāhiṅgukaliṅgagṛñjasauvarcalaiḥ
                        sātiviṣaiś ca cūrṇam |
                        <caesura/>
                         sukhāmbupītaṃ vinihantyajīrṇaṃ śūlaṃ visūcīmaruciṃ ca sadyaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.56.15"><l> kṣārāgadaṃ vā lavaṇaṃ viṣaṃ vā guḍapragāḍhān
                        atha sarṣapān vā |
                        <caesura/>
                         amlena vā saindhavahiṅguyuktau sabījapūrṇau saghṛtau trivargau || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.56.16"><l> kaṭutrikaṃ vā lavaṇair upetaṃ pibet
                        snuhīkṣīravimiśritaṃ tu |
                        <caesura/>
                         kalyāṇakaṃ vā lavaṇaṃ pibet tu yad uktam ādāv anilāmayeṣu || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.56.17"><l> kṛṣṇājamodakṣavakāṇi vāpi tulyau pibed vā
                        magadhānikumbhau |
                        <caesura/>
                         dantīyuta vā magadhodbhavānāṃ kalkaṃ pibet koṣavatīrasena || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.56.18"><l> uṣṇābhir adbhir magadhodbhavānāṃ kalkaṃ piben
                        nāgarakalkayuktam |
                        <caesura/>
                        <pb facs="867" n="783a"/> vyoṣaṃ karañjasya phalaṃ haridre mūlaṃ   
                     samaṃ cāpy atha mātuluṅgyāḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.56.19"><l> chāyāviśuṣkā guṭikāḥ kṛtās tā hanyur visūcīṃ
                        nayanāñjanena |
                        <caesura/>
                         suvāmitaṃ sādhuvirecitaṃ vā sulaṅghataṃ vā manujaṃ viditvā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.56.20"><l> peyādibhir dīpanapācanīyaiḥ samyakkṣudhārtaṃ
                        samupakrameta |
                        <caesura/>
                         āmaṃ śakṛd vā nicitaṃ krameṇa bhūyo vibaddhaṃ viguṇānilena || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.56.21"><l> pravartamānaṃ na yathāsvamenaṃ vikāram ānāham
                        udāharanti ||
                        <caesura/>
                         tasmin bhavantyāmasamudbhave tu tṛṣṇāpratiśyāyaśirovidāhāḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.56.22"><l> āmāśaye śūlam atho gurutvaṃ hṛllāsa
                        udgāravighātanaṃ ca |
                        <caesura/>
                        <pb facs="867" n="783b"/> stambhaḥ kaṭīpṛṣṭhapurīṣamūtre śūlo 'tha    
                    mūrcchā sa śakṛd vamec ca || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.56.23"><l> śvāsaś ca pakvāśayaje bhavanti liṅgāni
                        cātrālasakodbhavāni |
                        <caesura/>
                         āmodbhave vāntam upakrameta saṃsargabhaktakramadīpanīyaiḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.56.24"><l> athetaraṃ yo na śakṛd vamet tam āmaṃ jayet
                        svedanapācanaiś ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         visūcikāyāṃ parikīrtitāni dravyāṇi vairecanikāni yāni || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.56.25"><l> tānyeva vartīr viraced vicūrṇya
                        mahiṣyajāvībhagavāṃ tu mūtraiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         svinnasya pāyau viniveśya tāś ca cūrṇāni caiṣāṃ pradhamet tu
                        nāḍyā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.56.26"><l><pb facs="868" n="784b"/> mūtreṣu saṃsādhya                         yathāvidhānaṃ dravyāṇi yāny
ūrdhvam adhaś ca yānti |
                        <caesura/>
                         kvāthena tenāśu nirūhayec ca mūtrārdhayuktena samākṣikeṇa || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.56.27"><l> tribhaṇḍiyuktaṃ lavaṇaprakuñcaṃ dattvā
                        viriktakramam ācarec ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         eṣv eva tailena ca sādhitena prāptaṃ yadi syād anuvāsayec ca
                        ||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.56.trailer"><l>iti suśrutasaṃhitāyām uttaratantre
                        kāyacikitsātantre visūcikāpratiṣedho nāma (aṣṭādaśo 'dhyāyaḥ,
                        āditaḥ) ṣaṭpañcāśattamo 'dhyāyaḥ ||56 ||</l></lg>
                </div>



                <div n="57" type="adhyāya">
                    <head>saptapañcāśattamo 'dhyāyaḥ | </head>
                    <pb facs="868" n="784b"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.57.1"><l> athāto 'rocakapratiṣedham adhyāyaṃ
                        vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.57.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.57.3"><l> doṣaiḥ pṛthak saha ca cittaviparyayāc ca
                        bhaktāyaneṣu hṛdi cāvatate pragāḍham |
                        <caesura/>
                         nānne rucir bhavati taṃ bhiṣajo vikāraṃ bhaktopaghātam iha
                        pañcavidhaṃ vadanti || | </l></lg>
                    <l/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.57.4"><l> hṛcchūlapīḍanayutaṃ virasānanatvaṃ vātātmake
                        bhavati liṅgamarocake tu |
                        <caesura/>
                         hṛddāhacoṣabahutā mukhatiktatā ca mūrcchā satṛḍ bhavati pittakṛte
                        tathaiva || </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="869" n="785a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.57.5"><l> kaṇḍūgutvakaphasaṃsravasādatandrāḥ śleṣmātmake
                        madhuramāsyamarocake tu |
                        <caesura/>
                         sarvātmake pavanapittakaphā bahūni rūpāṇyathāsya hṛdaye
                        samudīrayanti || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.57.6"><l> saṃrāgaśokabhayaviplutacetasas tu cintākṛto
                        bhavati so 'śucidarśanāc ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         vāte vacāmbuvamanaṃ kṛtavān pibec ca snehaiḥ surābhir
                        athavoṣṇajalena cūrṇam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.57.7"><l>
                        kṛṣṇāviḍaṅgayavabhasmahareṇubhārgīrāsnailahiṅgulavaṇottamanāgarāṇām
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         pitte guḍāmbumadhurair vamanaṃ praśastaṃ snehaḥ
                        sasaindhavasitāmadhusarpiriṣṭaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.57.8"><l> nimbāmbuvāmitavataḥ kaphaje 'nupānaṃ
                        rājadrumāmbu madhunā tu sadīpyakaṃ syāt |
                        <caesura/>
                         cūrṇaṃ yad uktam athavānilaje tad eva sarvaiś ca sarvakṛtam evam
                        upakrameta || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.57.9"><l>
                        drākṣāpaṭolaviḍavetrakarīranimbamūrvābhayākṣabadarāmalakendravṛkṣaiḥ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         bījaiḥ karañjanṛpavṛkṣabhavaiś ca piṣṭair lehaṃ pacet
                        surabhimūtrayutaṃ yathāvat || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.57.10"><l> mustāṃ vacāṃ trikaṭukaṃ rajanīdvayaṃ ca
                        bhārgīṃ ca kuṣṭhamatha nirdahanīṃ ca piṣṭvā |
                        <caesura/>
                         mūtre 'vije dviradamūtrayute paced vā pāṭhāṃ tugāmativiṣāṃ
                        rajanīṃ ca mukhyām || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.57.11"><l> maṇḍūkimarkamamṛtāṃ ca salāṅgalākhyāṃ mūtre
                        pacet tu mahiṣasya vidhānavidvā |
                        <caesura/>
                         etān na santi caturo lihatas tu lehān
                        gulmāruciśvasanakaṇṭhahṛdāmayāś ca || </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="870" n="786a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.57.12"><l> sātmyān svadeśaracitān vividhāṃś ca bhakṣyān
                        pānāni mūlaphalaṣāḍavarāgayogān |
                        <caesura/>
                         adyādrasāṃś ca vividhān vividhaiḥ prakārair bhuñjīta cāpi
                        laghurūkṣamanaḥsukhāni || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.57.13"><l> āsthāpanaṃ vidhivadatra virecanaṃ ca kuryān
                        mṛdūni śirasaś ca virecanāni |
                        <caesura/>
                         trīṇy ūṣaṇāni rajanītriphalāyutāni cūrṇīkṛtāni
                        yavaśūkavimiśritāni || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.57.14"><l> kṣaudrāyutāni vitaren mukhabodhanārtham anyāni
                        tiktakaṭukāni ca bheṣajāni |
                        <caesura/>
                         mustādirājataruvargadaśāṅgasiddhaiḥ kvāthair jayen madhuyutair
                        vividhaiś ca lehaiḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.57.15"><l> mūtrāsavairguḍakṛtaiś ca tathā tvariṣṭaiḥ
                        kṣārāsavaiś ca madhumādhavatulyagandhaiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         syādeṣa eva kaphavātahate vidhiś ca śāntiṃ gate hutabhuji
                        praśamāya tasya || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.57.16"><l> icchābhighātabhayaśokahate 'ntaragnau bhāvān
                        bhavāya vitaret khalu śakyarūpān |
                        <caesura/>
                         artheṣu cāpy apaciteṣu punarbhavāya paurāṇikaiḥ
                        śrutiśatairanumānayettam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.57.17"><l> dainyaṃ gate manasi bodhanamatra śastaṃ yadyat
                        priyaṃ tadupasevyamarocake tu ||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.6.57.trailer"><l>iti suśrutasaṃhitāyām uttaratantrāntargate
                        kāyacikitsātantre 'rocakapratiṣedho nāma (ekonaviṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ,
                        āditaḥ) saptapañcāśattamo 'dhyāyaḥ ||57 ||</l></lg>
                </div>









   </div>         </body>
